Proper fix: resolve each manifest app in the destination by display name and/or a source marker stamped at create time (the verifier notes `api://{new}` is not derivable from the manifest), skip matches as "already present" and wire only new ones; add a planner unit test and a GUI test that the button disappears.

#### F037 · medium · effort M · Restore is not idempotent, yet its own report instructs the operator to re-run it
`apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands/restore.rs:751` · confirmed
**Problem.** The ManualItem for a missing managed identity says "Recreate it via your infrastructure-as-code, then re-run the restore", and backup-and-restore.md:203 repeats it, while Pass 1 creates a shell and mints fresh secrets for every manifest app on every run. Following the instruction duplicates the estate. Medium because the confirm modal states the restore creates new registrations and does not overwrite.
**Proposal.** Reword the ManualItem and the arch doc; add a "Re-bind managed identities only" action that runs just Pass 5 against a loaded manifest, and make `plan_restore` warn when `!tenant_changed`. Adoption via a marker is a larger design change (the core `Application` model has no `tags` field) better framed as an enhancement.

#### F038 · medium · effort S · Restore reports a failed Graph read as a confident runbook fact ('not found in destination' / 'app had none in the backup')
`apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands/restore.rs:734` · confirmed
**Problem.** `list_managed_identities().await.unwrap_or_default()` turns any `Err` into an empty map, so every MI is reported "not found in the destination, recreate via IaC"; `get_service_principal_by_app_id` collapses `Ok(None)` and `Err(_)` into "the app had none in the backup". Neither `restore_enterprise_app` nor `restore_managed_identities` takes `&SessionDead` or calls `note_code`, so a session dying in passes 4-5 produces a page of wrong runbook items and `session_expired` stays false.
**Proposal.** Match on the list result, `note_code` the error, push one warning "could not enumerate destination managed identities" and return; split the SP arm into `Ok(None)` (current text) and `Err(e)` ("could not read service principal"); add `note_code` to the tag, patch, assign and member failures in Pass 4; wiremock test with a 500 on `/servicePrincipals`.

#### F152 · medium · effort S · rewrite_identifier_uris only rewrites the exact `api://{appId}` form
`crates/azapptoolkit-core/src/restore_plan.rs:176` · confirmed (the verifier locates the helper at `restore_plan.rs:50-61`; `:176` is inside the tests)
**Problem.** The helper replaces only `u == "api://{source_app_id}"`. Microsoft supports `api://<tenantId>/<appId>`, `api://<tenantId>/<string>` and `api://<string>/<appId>`, and a GUID must match the app ID or tenant ID, so the source-GUID forms are rejected by the destination. The URIs share one `patch_application_expose_api` call with scopes and pre-authorized clients, so the whole Expose-an-API surface is lost with a single warning. The comment at `:151-154` calling the appId form "the ONLY one that encodes an identity" is incomplete.
**Proposal.** Take `source_tenant_id` and `dest_tenant_id` too and rewrite every `api://` segment equal to the source appId or source tenant GUID (case-insensitive), leaving `https://` and verified-domain forms untouched; add table tests for the three forms and a substring non-match; update backup-and-restore.md:148.

#### F150 · medium · effort M · FederatedIdentityCredential.subject is a bare String; a flexible FIC (subject: null) fails the whole list and skips the app from backup
`crates/azapptoolkit-core/src/models.rs:321` · confirmed
**Problem.** Flexible FICs (`claimsMatchingExpression`) require `subject` to be null, and `Paged<FederatedIdentityCredential>` is deserialized as one document, so a single flexible FIC fails `list_federated_credentials` with "invalid type: null, expected a string". The Federated credentials tab errors and the DR backup records the whole application as skipped. The repo already fixed this class for `AppRole.value` and `OAuth2PermissionGrant.scope`. Medium because flexible FICs are preview and the failure is loud.
**Proposal.** Make `subject: Option<String>` with `#[serde(default)]`, add `claims_matching_expression: Option<..>`, add a null-subject test, render the row as "expression-matched (flexible)", and in restore skip only that credential with a warning instead of the app.

- **F052 · `save_backup_to_file` / `save_restore_report_to_file` write `{}` on a serialization failure instead of erroring** — `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands/restore.rs:354` · confirmed. `serde_json::to_string_pretty(..).unwrap_or_else(|_| "{}")` would silently write an empty object and return success for the only file holding the show-once secrets. Serialize before calling `save_export_via_dialog` and `?` the error at the two DR sites (the helper has eight callers).
- **F043 · Restore regenerates a live secret for every backed-up password credential, including ones already expired at backup time** — `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands/restore.rs:588` · partially (restore.rs:78-81 documents minting a fresh window anyway, and a `Utc::now()` cutoff would be wrong for DR). Skip secrets whose `end_date_time` precedes `backup.created_at`, warn per skipped secret, and count them in the plan.

### Auth, session and token handling

#### F105 · high · effort M · Domain-form tenant id is accepted by the config screen but can never sign in (tid check compares a GUID to the domain)
`crates/azapptoolkit-auth/src/service/mod.rs:313` · confirmed, re-verified · also reported as F284 (same line; that verifier rated it medium: a functional first-run bug with the GUID as workaround)
**Problem.** `is_valid_tenant` accepts any dotted whitespace-free string and the config screen advertises "GUID or contoso.onmicrosoft.com", but nothing resolves a domain to its GUID (the only openid-configuration hit is a static URL formatter in `commands/sso`). The id_token `tid` is always a GUID, so `tenant_id != self.tenant_id` fails every sign-in with a `token_exchange` hint pointing at the wrong cause. `UserSettings::remembered_account_for` does the same string compare, so launch restore would never fire either.
```
if tenant_id != self.tenant_id {
```
**Proposal.** Either resolve a non-GUID tenant lazily in `sign_in` via `{auth_root}/{tenant}/v2.0/.well-known/openid-configuration` and persist the GUID (not in the synchronous `AppState::new`), or make `is_valid_tenant` GUID-only and align `config.rs:44` and `config_screen.rs:125/176-177` with the README. Add a test with a non-GUID configured tenant.

#### F106 · medium · effort S · sign_out clears in-memory session before the fallible keyring delete
`crates/azapptoolkit-auth/src/service/mod.rs:561` · confirmed
**Problem.** `sign_out` invalidates the token cache and removes `known_tenants` before `delete_refresh_token(..)?`; on a keyring failure the command returns before `forget_account()` and `state.cache.invalidate_tenant`. Every later command fails `not_signed_in` while the toast says "you are still signed in", and `last_account` plus the keyring token survive, so the next launch restores the session the operator tried to end. Medium because it needs a keyring failure at that moment and a retry re-runs the path.
**Proposal.** Delete the refresh token first (via `spawn_blocking`) and only then clear `cache`/`known_tenants`; in the command run `forget_account()`, client removal and cache invalidation unconditionally and return the keyring error afterwards so the toast can say the OS store still holds a token. Add a service test with a failing `delete_credential` fake.

#### F107 · medium · effort M · CAE-ness is not part of the token cache key, and every interactive/restore flow seeds the Graph slots with non-CAE tokens
`crates/azapptoolkit-auth/src/service/mod.rs:581` · confirmed
**Problem.** `graph_for` builds every Graph adapter with `new_cae`, but `sign_in`, `consent_for_scopes`, `reauthenticate`, `refresh_session` and `restore_session` all seed the same `(tenant, scope_key)` slots with tokens minted without `claims`, and `run_auth_code_flow`/`authorize_url` never send `claims`. state.rs:563-565 states the invariant this violates. After launch, Refresh Token, Grant consent or reauth the Graph adapters serve a non-CAE token for its full lifetime, losing prompt revocation.
**Proposal.** Fold CAE-ness into the cache key (`TokenCache::get/put` take `cae: bool`), then mint CAE for Graph audiences in the five flows (`claims = build_cae_claims(None)` on `/authorize` and `/token`; `access_token_for_scopes_cae` in refresh and restore); wiremock test that the `claims` form field is present and that `graph_for` consumes the CAE slot.

#### F108 · medium · effort M · `interaction_required` / `login_required` are classified as a dead refresh token and purge the whole session
`crates/azapptoolkit-auth/src/service/wire.rs:55` · confirmed
**Problem.** `wire.rs:55-60` maps `interaction_required`/`login_required` to `InvalidGrant`, and `access_token_inner` then deletes the keyring token, all cached access tokens and the `known_tenants` entry. MSAL treats these as a per-resource step-up with the RT still valid. A first ARM, Exchange or Log Analytics call under a resource-scoped MFA policy therefore kills Graph browsing too; the frontend's own hint table already says "complete the MFA prompt". The loop claim holds only when the policy is scoped to the non-Graph resource.
**Proposal.** Add `AuthError::InteractionRequired` (UI code `interaction_required`, not re-auth-fatal) for these errors and for `invalid_grant` with AADSTS 50074/50076/50079/50158, do not purge (mirror the `ConsentRequired` arm), generalise `consent_for_scopes` into `interactive_for_scopes(tenant, scopes, prompt)` and offer "Verify identity"; wiremock tests that the keyring token survives and 70008 still purges.

#### F021 · medium · effort S · Readiness scope probe keeps a hardcoded CAE list that has drifted from the capabilities catalog
`apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands/readiness.rs:276` · confirmed
**Problem.** `is_graph` matches `"write" | "sync" | "audit_log" | "policy" | "policy_write" | "sharepoint"`; the catalog carries `group_membership` and `ensure_group_member_token` acquires it with `cae = true`, so the readiness page pre-warms a non-CAE token that the `new_cae` group-member adapter then reuses. `sync` and `policy_write` have no catalog `scope_feature`, showing hand maintenance.
**Proposal.** Co-locate CAE-ness with the scope set: have `consent_scopes_for` return `(Vec<String>, bool)` and make the `ensure_scoped_token` wrappers and `probe_scope` read it; delete the `is_graph` match; add a test that every catalog `scope_feature` resolves through it.

#### F253 · medium · effort S · Group-membership token requests only GroupMember.ReadWrite.All; Learn documents that adding a servicePrincipal member also requires Application.ReadWrite.All
`crates/azapptoolkit-auth/src/service/scopes.rs:79` · confirmed
**Problem.** The member the app always adds is a service principal, exactly the row Learn's "Add members" table singles out as needing both scopes. The dedicated token is minted from a single-scope request, the catalog row lists only GroupMember, and readiness reports the scope half as satisfied. Runtime enforcement could not be verified offline, but `Application.ReadWrite.All` is already in the write bundle, so requesting it widens nothing.
**Proposal.** Change `default_graph_group_member_scopes` to request both, list both in the `group_membership` capability and its remediation string, and add a scopes.rs test mirroring `write_scopes_cover_mutations`.

#### F254 · medium · effort S · Claims-mapping policy assign/list ride a Policy-only token; Learn requires Application.ReadWrite.All AND a Policy.* scope
`crates/azapptoolkit-graph/src/client/policies.rs:26` · confirmed
**Problem.** The `$ref` assign, list and remove operations on the service principal use `policy_write_token`, minted with `Policy.ReadWrite.ApplicationConfiguration` alone; Learn requires `Application.ReadWrite.All` in the same token (only `POST /policies/claimsMappingPolicies` is Policy-only). `get_sso_config` calls the list on every SSO-tab open and degrades. The verifier notes a browse-only path still needs `Application.ReadWrite.All`, so the gain is avoiding the write Policy scope on reads only.
**Proposal.** Mint the policy-write token with both scopes and document the pair in a capability entry; consider routing `list_assigned_claims_mapping_policies` through `Policy.Read.All` plus the write bundle.

#### F353 · medium · effort S · `CommandState::run` (inline-error path, 24 call sites) never offers the dead-session Re-authenticate recovery
`apps/desktop/web-rs/src/hooks/use_command.rs:85` · confirmed
**Problem.** `run`'s `on_err` calls only `report_consent_required` then sets the inline error, while `run_toast_err` routes through `report_command_error_for`, which tries `report_if_session_dead` first. The sink's own doc says inline-affordance surfaces must call it first. Every form modal or tab mutation on an expired session shows the raw `refresh_missing` text in red with no Re-authenticate action.
**Proposal.** Call `session.report_if_session_dead(&e)` before `report_consent_required` in `run`'s `on_err` (keep the inline text); add a host unit test that `refresh_missing` pushes a toast with `action_label == Some("Re-authenticate")`.

#### F165 · medium · effort S · Mid-pagination errors are flattened to Protocol, losing auth/throttle classification and the re-auth-fatal signal
`crates/azapptoolkit-exchange/src/client/transport.rs:78` · confirmed
**Problem.** The `page > 1` arm reclassifies every error to `ExchangeError::Protocol`, though the comment justifies it only for NotFound. `bearer()` runs on every page, so a `Token{refresh_missing}` on page 2 becomes `protocol_error`, `is_reauth_fatal` is false, and the audit's Exchange probes or bulk remediations keep running on a dead session; a page-2 401/403 also misses the audit breaker.
**Proposal.** Reclassify only `NotFound` and `Api { body }` where `is_not_found_body` holds; return everything else unchanged; add wiremock tests for a page-2 401 and a page-2 `refresh_missing` token failure.

- **F121 · InvalidGrant purge is unconditional, so a refresh still in flight across a completed reauthenticate can delete the new refresh token** — `crates/azapptoolkit-auth/src/service/mod.rs:529` · partially (needs a `/token` POST stalled for the whole browser round trip, so far rarer than "every reauth"). Keep a `session_generation` counter bumped on store and skip the purge when it changed, or compare the loaded RT before deleting.
- **F210 · Blocking keyring delete runs inline on the tokio worker (and under the refresh lock)** — `crates/azapptoolkit-auth/src/service/mod.rs:535` · confirmed. save and load are wrapped in `spawn_blocking` with a rationale, but `delete_refresh_token` is called inline at `:535` and `:564` (the clippy `unused_async` hit on `sign_out`). Wrap both in `spawn_blocking` via a private helper.
- **F109 · Loopback honours an `error=` redirect before validating `state`, and writes the success page before the state check** — `crates/azapptoolkit-auth/src/service/loopback.rs:86` · confirmed · also reported as F289 (`loopback.rs:99`, partially: RFC 8252 does not literally prescribe ignoring mismatched requests, and continuing the loop trades a fast fail for the 300 s timeout). Any local process or web page can abort the sign-in and inject an `error` string; `error_description` is dropped so the AADSTS code never reaches `aadsts_hint`. Validate `state` first, answer 400 and `continue` on mismatch, whitelist the surfaced `error`, and update `state_mismatch_is_rejected`.
- **F003 · global_search (and prefetch_search_corpus / get_directory_index_status) answer from cache without proving the session** — `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands/search.rs:302` · confirmed. `graph_for` only builds adapters, so a warm corpus is served on a dead session instead of raising `not_signed_in`, and the cache invariant accepts `graph_for(` as a proof. Add `prove_tenant_session` to the three commands and drop `graph_for(` from `SESSION_PROOFS`.
- **F288 · `TokenResponse` derives Debug with plaintext access, refresh and id tokens** — `crates/azapptoolkit-auth/src/service/wire.rs:13` · confirmed. It is the one secret-bearing type without a redacted Debug; no call site prints it today. Hand-write `Debug` printing `<redacted>` for the three tokens and add the mirror test.

### Configuration, cloud environment and updater controls

#### F036 · high · effort S · Documented auto-update opt-out (AZAPPTOOLKIT_AUTO_UPDATE / settings.json auto_update) is never read; launch check always runs
`apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands/updater.rs:26` · confirmed, re-verified · also reported as F139 (same line)
**Problem.** `UserSettings::load`, the only place `auto_update_env_override` is applied, has no production caller; every production reader uses `UserSettings::stored`. Neither `check_for_update`, `perform_update` nor the frontend consults `auto_update`, and `shell.rs` fires the check on every mount. README.md:262-283 promises "no network calls to the updater endpoint at any point in the session" and tells MSI deployers to rely on the switch; DEVELOPMENT.md cites a settings toggle that does not exist. Installation still needs the operator to click "Update & restart", so the harm is unexpected egress plus a misleading prompt.
**Proposal.** Gate both commands on `UserSettings::load(&config_directory()).auto_update`: `check_for_update` returns `Ok(None)` without network and `perform_update` returns a typed `updates_disabled` error; expose the policy to the UI so the account menu hides the manual item with a reason; add a settings test for the env var, a src-tauri short-circuit test, and either implement or delete the DEVELOPMENT.md toggle clause.

#### F252 · medium · effort S · SSO wizard hardcodes the global-cloud application template id and login host; sovereign clouds fail
`apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands/sso/mod.rs:41` · confirmed · also reported as F287 (`sso/mod.rs:44`, the `LOGIN_HOST` half and the federation-metadata probe)
**Problem.** `CUSTOM_TEMPLATE_ID` and `LOGIN_HOST` are commercial-only constants while every other host rides `CloudEnvironment`. Learn lists distinct non-gallery template ids for US Government and China, so `instantiate_application_template` 404s there, and the SAML/OIDC Login, Logout and Metadata URLs and `probe_federation_metadata` point at `login.microsoftonline.com`, so every staged rollover in a sovereign tenant reads "couldn't check". The SAML issuer `sts.windows.net` is also China-specific. DoD sharing the US Gov template id is an inference.
**Proposal.** Add `CloudEnvironment::custom_app_template_id()` and `saml_issuer_root()`, thread `state.auth.cloud()` into `saml_summary_urls`/`oidc_summary_urls` using `login_authority_root()`, replace the constants at `:90-100`, `:167` and `:313`; validate `app_id` with `is_guid` before building the metadata URL; add per-cloud tests.

#### F141 · medium · effort S · UserSettings::mutate overwrites an unparseable or unreadable settings.json with defaults
`crates/azapptoolkit-core/src/settings.rs:121` · confirmed
**Problem.** `mutate` starts from `Self::stored`, which is `from_file(..).unwrap_or_default()`, and `from_file` collapses both an I/O error (EACCES, EIO, AV lock) and a parse error into `None` with a `warn!`. `save_locked` then serialises defaults over the file. `remember_account` runs `mutate` on every sign-in, so a hand-edit typo (which defaults.rs anticipates) wipes `tenant_defaults` including `app_vaults` with no recovery. private_file.rs:39-41 names this loss but closed only the torn-write cause.
**Proposal.** Make `from_file` return `Result<Option<Self>, io::Error>` distinguishing NotFound from parse/IO failures; keep `stored()` lenient for readers but have `mutate` refuse, or rename the file to `settings.json.corrupt-<ts>` and log at `error!` before writing defaults. Test that `not json` survives a `mutate`.

### Security hardening

#### F426 · medium · effort S · Key Vault browser: listed secrets, vault name and `loaded` survive a tenant switch/sign-out
`apps/desktop/web-rs/src/views/key_vault_view.rs:42` · confirmed
**Problem.** The only Effect tracks `session.view` and wipes `revealed`/`error`; `vault_name`, `listed` and `loaded` have no tenant dependency, and `VaultPicker` resets `all_vaults` but not `value`. After `set_active_tenant`, tenant A's secret names, content types, expiry dates and vault name are still on screen in tenant B, and Reveal sends `kv_get_secret(tenantB, vaultA, name)`. Every sibling panel resets on `tenant.get()` with an explicit "cross-tenant leakage" comment. What leaks is metadata, not values.
**Proposal.** Add an `Effect` on `session.active_tenant` clearing all five signals (the verifier prefers this over moving a `Vec<KvSecretItemDto>` into `TenantScopedUi`), plus a GUI test: list secrets, switch tenant, assert the table is empty and the field blank.

- **F044 · Webview grants wider than used: connect-src external hosts and updater/dialog capabilities are backend-only concerns** — `apps/desktop/src-tauri/tauri.conf.json:25` · partially (tokens never reach the webview and updater payloads are signature-verified, so this is least-privilege hygiene) · also reported as F156 (confirmed) and F286 (partially: the "browser cookies" exposure does not apply, since sign-in runs in the system browser). The CSP allowlists 13 Microsoft origins that the WASM frontend, which has no HTTP client, never contacts, contrary to AGENTS.md's own rule. Reduce to `connect-src 'self'` (Tauri appends its IPC origins), verify under `just dev`, and pin it with a repo_invariants test.
- **F292 · Webview capability grants `updater:default` and `dialog:allow-save` that no frontend code invokes** — `apps/desktop/src-tauri/capabilities/default.json:10` · confirmed. Both plugins are driven from Rust `AppHandle` APIs that are not capability-gated, and `updater:default` exposes `plugin:updater|download_and_install`, the silent-install path AGENTS.md forbids. Remove both, exercise export, backup and update under `just dev`, and assert the permission list in a repo_invariants test.
- **F285 · Updater signing key is in the environment of the whole `cargo tauri build`** — `.github/workflows/release.yml:142` · partially (rust-cache keys on `github.job`, so CI caches do not reach the release job, and a malicious build script already controls the binary being signed). Every build.rs and proc-macro runs with `TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY` in env. Split the recipes into `cargo tauri build --no-bundle` then `cargo tauri bundle --config updater-build.json` and scope the key to the bundle step, updating DEVELOPMENT.md:290-296.
- **F045 · rcgen `KeyPair` holds an un-zeroized copy of the PKCS#8 private key; the `zeroize` feature is not enabled** — `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/cert.rs:106` · confirmed. `GeneratedCert::drop` wipes its own three fields but `key_pair.serialized_der` is freed unwiped. Enable rcgen's `zeroize` feature (zeroize is already a direct dependency) and call `key_pair.zeroize()` after `build_pfx`.
- **F142 · private_file temp path is predictable and opened without O_EXCL** — `crates/azapptoolkit-core/src/private_file.rs:67` · partially (needs a unix host, a shared export directory, the victim's pid and a to-the-second filename; Windows needs symlink privilege). `<target>.tmp<pid>` is opened with `create(true).truncate(true)`, so a pre-planted symlink is followed and then renamed into place. Open with `create_new(true)` plus a counter suffix and add a unix-only symlink test.
- **F291 · Log (and config) directory is created with the process umask while every file the app writes is owner-only** — `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/lib.rs:339` · partially (macOS `~/Library` is already 0700 and many Linux homes are 0750, so exposure is limited to 755-home setups). Logs carry tenant ids, scope sets and Graph error bodies. Create both directories with mode 0o700 on unix and tighten existing ones.
- **F293 · Release-note markdown links render any `href` scheme, and the update splash feeds it the unsigned `latest.json` `notes` field** — `apps/desktop/web-rs/src/components/changelog_notes.rs:376` · confirmed. The minisign signature covers the bundle, not `latest.json`; CSP blocks `javascript:` today, so this is defense in depth. Accept only `https://` (optionally `http://`) hrefs, render others as text, and note in release-updater-demo.md that `notes` is unsigned.
- **F290 · Graph/ARM/Key Vault error bodies flow raw and uncapped into errors, logs and the UI** — `crates/azapptoolkit-graph/src/client/transport.rs:597` · confirmed. Only the Exchange client control-char-strips and length-caps bodies; a proxy block page or multi-MB HTML error lands verbatim in toasts and the 14-day log. Move `sanitize_error_body` into `azapptoolkit-core` and call it at the four body-capture sites.
- **F208 · `SecretValue` derives `Serialize` although nothing serialises it** — `crates/azapptoolkit-keyvault/src/models.rs:41` · confirmed. The hand-written redacting `Debug` can be bypassed by `serde_json::to_string` or a JSON tracing layer; the IPC boundary uses `KvSecretValueDto`. Remove `Serialize` and keep `Deserialize`.
- **F204 · `LogAnalyticsClient::query` and the ARM list calls splice server-supplied ids into URL paths with no structural check** — `crates/azapptoolkit-arm/src/loganalytics.rs:51` · confirmed · also reported as F294 (same line). `customerId`, `subscriptionId` and the vault `scope` from ARM responses reach `format!` unchecked while `get_role_definition` refuses the same class; the host is fixed, so impact is path or query reshaping. Add a `require_guid` helper in core (the desktop-crate `is_guid` is unreachable from arm) plus an absolute-path check for `scope`, with wiremock refusal tests.
- **F234 · Azure custom role grants Key Vault deleteSecret that the app never calls; docs claim "full secret CRUD"** — `docs/operator-rbac/azure-custom-role.json:19` · confirmed. The keyvault client exposes only list, get and set; the only `delete_secret` mention is a stale doc comment. Remove the DataAction, reword OPERATOR-ROLES.md:31, :53 and :194, and fix `keyvault/src/lib.rs:10`.

### Cache freshness

#### F041 · medium · effort S · Managed Identities 'Refresh' invalidates only `{tenant}|mi`, but the list is rebuilt from the untouched shared SP index
`apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands/diagnostics.rs:97` · confirmed
**Problem.** `list_managed_identities` was refactored to derive from `sp_index_cached`, but the `ManagedIdentities` arm of `invalidate_list_cache` drops only the `{tenant_id}|mi` prefix, which is immediately re-derived from the still-cached index. The Apps and Enterprise arms both also drop `sp_index_key`, with a comment saying why. An MI created seconds ago stays absent until the 1 h TTL or a refresh on another page.
**Proposal.** Also invalidate `sp_index_key(&tenant_id)` and `search_corpus_key` in the `ManagedIdentities` arm; add a repo_invariants rule or unit test that every `ListCacheKindDto` arm backing a list derived from `sp_index_cached` drops the index.

#### F005 · medium · effort S · list_app_role_resources is cached with "no mutation-driven invalidation" although the App roles tab mutates exactly what it lists
`apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands/permissions.rs:68` · confirmed
**Problem.** The doc comment calls the list a read-only directory, but `upsert_enterprise_app_role`/`delete_enterprise_app_role` change which SPs expose an enabled Application role, and create/delete application add or remove the SP. None drop `{tenant}|app_role_resources`, so the Grant-access picker omits a freshly published API, keeps a deleted app and shows stale counts for up to 60 minutes; the picker has no Refresh affordance. The graph crate busts its own `resource:` prefix on the same writes.
**Proposal.** Move `app_role_resources_key` into `applications/cache.rs`, drop it inside `invalidate_app_lists`, add a targeted invalidate on the Ok path of the two app-role mutations, extend `detail_cache_tests`, and fix the doc comment.

#### F332 · medium · effort S · List Refresh bumps the resource before the cache invalidation is awaited, so it can refetch the cached list
`apps/desktop/web-rs/src/views/application_list.rs:245` · confirmed
**Problem.** `on_refresh` bumps `reload` and only then spawns `invalidate_list_cache`; the backend's cache-hit path returns synchronously, so nothing orders the two IPCs and Refresh can show the same stale rows. `enterprise_application_list.rs:114-123` has the same shape, while the detail pane and both managed-identity refreshes correctly await the invalidate first.
**Proposal.** Move `reload.update(..)` inside the spawned task after `invalidate_list_cache(..).await` in both lists, mirroring `application_detail_pane.rs:70-73`; optionally a GUI test whose mock records call order.

- **F006 · list_credential_expirations stores a tenant-wide scan unguarded** — `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands/credentials.rs:249` · confirmed. A credential mutation during the multi-second scan drops the key, then the pre-mutation snapshot is stored with plain `cache.put`, the store-after-invalidate race the doc records as fixed for the other list caches; the invariant counts only `put_index(`. Capture `generation_for` before the scan, store via `put_index_if_current`, and extend the invariant.
- **F080 · assign_managed_identity_azure_role never busts the Key Vault access sweep cache** — `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands/managed_identity.rs:390` · partially (a vault-path-only condition would be too narrow, since `atScope()` includes inherited scopes). A role assigned by the app changes "who can touch this vault?" but `get_cached_key_vault_access` serves the pre-assignment sweep until TTL. Make `kv_sweep_cache_key` `pub(crate)` and invalidate it unconditionally on Ok; pin the pairing in `repo_invariants/cache.rs`.

### Robustness, error classification and UI state

#### F425 · medium · effort S · `invoke_result` unwraps the error deserialization; any rejection that is not a `UiError` JSON object panics the whole WASM app
`apps/desktop/web-rs/src/bindings/mod.rs:17` · confirmed
**Problem.** tauri-sys `invoke_result` unwraps `swb::from_value(err)`, and `UiError` is a struct, so Tauri's plain-string rejections (invalid args, unknown command, denied plugin permission) fail to deserialize and hit `console_error_panic_hook`, freezing the UI with no toast. The repo rule presents `invoke_result` as the safe path; nothing pins binding arg names to backend params, and the GUI mock only ever rejects with a `UiError`.
```
.map_err(|err| swb::from_value(err).unwrap())
```
**Proposal.** Add `bindings::ipc::invoke_result<T>` that deserializes both sides via `serde_json::Value` and maps failures to `UiError::new("ipc", value.to_string(), false)`; switch every binding to it and add a repo_invariants scan that bindings never import `tauri_sys::core::{invoke,invoke_result}` directly (allow-listing the intentionally infallible `invoke` calls).

#### F220 · medium · effort M · tauri-sys git pin predates an upstream workaround for a reported wasm-bindgen function-table exhaustion in event deserialization
`apps/desktop/web-rs/Cargo.toml:33` · partially (the issue is single-reporter, still open and unconfirmed by wasm-bindgen maintainers, and not reproduced in this repo; the original "high" was overstated)
**Problem.** The pinned rev calls `serde_wasm_bindgen::from_value(raw).unwrap()` inside listener closures; upstream commit 1ffc6fa (four commits later) routes through `JSON::stringify` plus `serde_json`, citing wasm-bindgen#5324 ("Out of bounds table access" after many events in a Tauri + Leptos app). This app consumes eight `listen::<..>` streams and the backend emits one event per app across tenant-wide audits and bulk runs, the exact shape reported. There is no crates.io release for Dependabot to track, so the pin rots silently.
**Proposal.** Bump to upstream v2 rev 1ffc6fa (or head), regenerate `web-rs/Cargo.lock`, run `just web-itest`; record that undeserializable events now `log::error!` instead of panicking; add a review date to the pin comment and optionally a `just deps-git-status` recipe.

#### F020 · medium · effort S · ConcurrencyThrottle halves only once under sustained 429 pressure because the window re-anchors on every event
`apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands/throttle.rs:102` · confirmed
**Problem.** `*last = Some(now)` runs before the `within_window` early return, so every suppressed 429 slides the anchor; with lanes receiving 429s more often than every 2 s the cap goes 8 to 4 and stays there, and the documented degrade-to-floor behaviour never engages. The only test fires bursts at one instant then advances past the window. A cap stuck at half under a storm burns retry budget and can turn into `unscored` degraded runs.
**Proposal.** Track `last_throttle` (every event, drives recovery) and `last_halved` (only when the cap shrinks, drives the window); add a paused-clock test firing `on_throttle` every 1 s for 10 s on `new(8)` asserting `MIN_CONCURRENCY`.

#### F180 · medium · effort S · SharePoint resolver reports 403/429/401 on the subsite probe as 'did not resolve to a list, library or item'
`crates/azapptoolkit-graph/src/client/sharepoint.rs:200` · confirmed
**Problem.** The subsite descent matches `Err(_)` and returns `GraphError::Protocol(".. did not resolve ..")`, though only a 404 means "not a subsite". `Forbidden`, `Unauthorized` and post-budget `Throttled` are all collapsed, so `map_sharepoint_err` never splices the `sharepoint_selected_items` remediation (it requires `code == "forbidden"`), `retryable` is false for a throttle, and the multi-target grant loop warns "could not resolve <url>", misdirecting the operator to fix the URL.
**Proposal.** Match `Err(GraphError::NotFound(_))` for the not-a-subsite branch and return every other error unchanged; add a wiremock test that a 403 on the descent URL yields `Forbidden`.

#### F352 · medium · effort S · `/` shortcut targets the first filter input in DOM order, so it no-ops once any earlier keep-alive pane has been visited
`apps/desktop/web-rs/src/hooks/use_shortcuts.rs:53` · confirmed
**Problem.** `focus_list_filter` uses `query_selector` (first match in document order) then bails if that element has no offset parent, instead of picking the first laid-out match as the comment says. Under keep-alive the App Registrations input precedes later views, so after one visit to Apps `/` does nothing on Enterprise Apps, Managed Identities or Security. Two of the three selector alternates match nothing (the only `search-input*` class is `search-input__clear`).
**Proposal.** Use `query_selector_all` on `input[placeholder^='Filter']` and pick the first with `offset_parent().is_some()`; drop or fix the dead alternates; add a GUI test with the first input inside `display:none`.

#### F335 · medium · effort S · `pending_app_tab` leaks to the next opened app when the deep-link target is already open
`apps/desktop/web-rs/src/state/navigation.rs:108` · confirmed · also reported as F351 (same line)
**Problem.** `open_app_on_tab` sets `pending_app_tab` unconditionally then calls `open_item`, which for an already-open `(kind, entity_id)` only retitles and refocuses; panes are keyed `<For>` keep-alive and consume the flag once on mount, so it is never consumed. The next unrelated app opened from the list lands on Credentials instead of `last_app_tab`. `open_enterprise_on_tab` has the identical leak; the MI variant is immune because it writes `last_mi_tab`.
**Proposal.** Only set the pending signal when `self.is_open(kind, &id).is_none()` in both helpers (an already-open pane keeps its live tab, as the MI doc says is intended), or add a per-open-item `requested_tab` the pane observes; add a unit test in `state/mod.rs`.

#### F412 · medium · effort M · Demo audit, credential-expiry and SSO rows use ids that don't match the demo catalog, so every "Open" lands on a "Demo App" placeholder
`apps/desktop/web-rs/src/ipc_mock/fixtures.rs:472` · confirmed
**Problem.** Fixture rows are keyed `obj-{name}`, `{name}-appid` and `sp-payroll` while the demo catalog ids are `guid("{name}:obj")`, and the args-aware `get_application_detail` mock falls back to `f::application_detail(oid, oid, "Demo App")`. On the published demo, Open from any Security finding, credential-expiry row or SSO-certificate row opens a placeholder pane; the finding-to-fix-to-open journey the demo exists to show is broken, and attached `remediations` target ids that exist nowhere.
**Proposal.** Add id-taking builders (`audit_item_with_ids`, `credential_row_for`) rather than changing `audit_item`/`credential_row` (GUI tests assert the current ids), and build the demo rows from `catalog()` via `obj_id`/`app_id`; for a consistency check, lift the catalog and pure builders into a feature-free module testable natively, since `demo`/`mock-ipc` are not enabled by `just web-test`.

- **F330 · `?` opens the shortcuts sheet over an open modal, stacking two ModalShells with the same id and two focus traps** — `apps/desktop/web-rs/src/hooks/use_shortcuts.rs:163` · confirmed. Bare-key handling is gated only by `is_typing`, ModalShell hard-codes `id="modal-shell-title"`, and `use_escape` has no topmost check so one Escape closes every dialog; the verifier counts 11 hand-rolled backdrops. Skip bare keys when `.modal-backdrop` exists, give ModalShell a per-instance id, and migrate the hand-rolled dialogs onto it.
- **F359 · Shortcut sheet <kbd> uses an undefined `--surface-alt` token (renders transparent) while the designed `.ui-kbd` rule sits unused** — `apps/desktop/web-rs/styles.css:4281` · confirmed. No `--surface-alt:` is defined anywhere, so the key chips have a border and no fill. Give the `<kbd>` `class="ui-kbd"` and delete the ad-hoc rule; close the token set for `--surface-hover` and `--border-subtle`.
- **F154 · Seven Vec fields use bare #[serde(default)] without null_to_default** — `crates/azapptoolkit-core/src/models.rs:180` · partially (no evidence Graph nulls these fields; cheap hardening, not a demonstrated failure). `allowed_member_types`, `group_types`, two `roles`, `granted_to_identities`, `categories` and `supported_single_sign_on_modes` would each fail a whole paged response on an explicit null. Add `deserialize_with = "null_to_default"` to the seven fields and extend the doc comment.
- **F048 · `set_tenant_defaults` blank-normalises two of three patterns; only direct IPC callers can observe the inconsistency** — `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands/defaults.rs:39` · partially (the Settings view trims and sends `None` for blank, and `secret_name_for` falls back at use, so the UI scenario is refuted). `secret_name_pattern` skips the `.map(trim).filter(!empty)` step its two siblings get. Apply the same normalisation and add a test.
- **F194 · Key Vault `retry_class_for` doc comment contradicts itself about `setSecret` (a PUT)** — `crates/azapptoolkit-keyvault/src/client.rs:267` · partially (the replay re-sends the identical value, so the duplicate version is correct and no credential is orphaned; routing PUT to NonIdempotent would make rotation more fragile). Rewrite the comment to state the deliberate same-value replay and optionally add a 502-then-200 wiremock test asserting identical bodies.
- **F087 · Operator-facing strings carry runs of ~18 spaces from missing `\` line continuations** — `crates/azapptoolkit-dto/src/audit.rs:199` · confirmed. `AuditCoverageGap::description()` at `:199`, `:207`, `:210`, `:213` and `sso/mod.rs:432` render in `pre-wrap` Callouts and the CSV export. Add the continuations and assert `!description().contains("  ")` for all six variants.

### Tooling and developer workflow

#### F295 · high · effort S · `just setup` panics on a fresh clone: `cargo check` runs before any frontend dist exists
`scripts/setup.sh:132` · confirmed, re-verified
**Problem.** With the default `custom-protocol` feature, Tauri's `generate_context!` panics at compile time when `apps/desktop/web-rs/dist` is absent, and `dist/` is gitignored. Both `setup.sh` (`set -euo pipefail`) and `setup.ps1` run `cargo check --workspace` before the `trunk build` that would create it, so the documented first command fails on all three OSes. The justfile already routes `check`, `clippy` and `test` through `_stub-frontend-dist`; the setup scripts predate that and also drop `--locked`. The panic names the missing path, so it is diagnosable.
**Proposal.** Replace the hand-typed `cargo check --workspace` with `just check` and run it after the `trunk build` step (or swap the order); change `trunk build` to `trunk build --locked`; fix the stale justfile:195 "committed" stub comment.

- **F307 · branch-switch-guard lets `git switch -` / `git checkout -` through** — `.claude/hooks/branch-switch-guard.sh:71` · partially (`@{-1}` passes the filter, and the stash-combo block is the documented two-step flow, a known false positive). `-` starts with `-`, so it yields no target and the switch is allowed on a dirty tree. Treat a bare `-` as a target and optionally skip the block after a `git stash` segment.
- **F308 · changelog-check nudges about the previous commit when the `git commit` it saw actually failed** — `.claude/hooks/changelog-check.sh:38` · confirmed. HEAD is essentially never unreadable, so after a failed commit the hook nudges about the prior commit. Only nudge when HEAD's committer date is within about 120 s.
- **F306 · secrets-scanner's gitleaks path ignores SCAN_SCOPE and labels every gitleaks failure as 'secrets detected'** — `.claude/hooks/secrets-scanner.sh:26` · partially (the timeout claim is speculative for this 3 MB history, and gitleaks exit 1 means "leaks or error", so exit codes cannot separate findings from tooling errors). Branch on `$scope` (`gitleaks git` for all, `--pre-commit --staged` plus `gitleaks dir` for diff) and word the message honestly.
- **F301 · `verify-full` has no Windows story, and the docs that advertise it as 'full CI parity' do not say so** — `justfile:146` · partially (the Unix-only choice is documented inside the justfile and in DEVELOPMENT.md:324-326; the gap is the AGENTS.md and DEVELOPMENT.md:90 parity claim). `web-itest-size` is a bash-shebang recipe with no `[windows]` twin and `verify-full` depends on `web-itest`, not `-auto`. Add `[unix]`/`[windows]` variants with the LOUD-skip text, or state the OS caveat in the docs.

---

## Cleanup, refactors, performance and dependencies

This bucket holds the 114 findings about maintainability rather than behaviour. They cover dead code and stale in-code documentation, and copies of logic or UI that an existing primitive already provides. They also cover oversized files, avoidable Graph and runtime work, and manifest hygiene. None is high severity; 14 are medium and 100 are low (83 confirmed, 31 partially confirmed). By category: 65 cleanup, 34 refactor, 10 performance, 5 dependency findings. Eight pairs describe the same issue at the same file and are merged below, so the section carries 106 entries. Effort is small for 90 findings; only the detail-pane rebuild (F336) is large.

### Dead code, stale comments and small cleanups

All findings here are low severity. The recurring pattern is a blanket lint allow or a stale comment that hides leftovers from a removed feature, plus a handful of small invariants enforced on one path but not another.

- **F093 · `#![allow(dead_code)]` on the whole bindings module hides five dead items** — `apps/desktop/web-rs/src/bindings/mod.rs:1` · confirmed. The blanket allow silences `ResolvePermissionArgs`, `ExportArgs`, `UpdateRequiredResourceArgs`, keyvault `SetArgs` and `is_tauri_runtime`, none referenced outside their definitions; the auth.rs header still names a removed `current_tenants` command. Delete the five items, drop the allow and fix the header; the verifier adds that demo/mod.rs:265 still mocks `current_tenants`.
- **F346 · Dead and duplicate IPC argument structs hidden by `#![allow(dead_code)]` on the bindings module** — `apps/desktop/web-rs/src/bindings/applications.rs:75` · confirmed. Three arg structs have no callers, `FederatedListArgs` duplicates `ObjectIdArgs`, and `TenantArgs` is redeclared in four files beside `common::TenantArg`. Delete the dead structs, use the common ones and narrow the allow; some bindings exist only for demo fixtures, so targeted allows may be needed.
- **F431 · Dead IPC leftovers hidden by `#![allow(dead_code)]`: `keyvault::SetArgs` for a non-existent `kv_set_secret`, unused `auth::is_tauri_runtime`, and six one-field tenant arg structs redeclared beside `common::TenantArg`** — `apps/desktop/web-rs/src/bindings/keyvault.rs:60` · partially (six redeclarations, not five; `RevokeOauth2ScopeArgs` is used and `remove_selected_item_permission` is an unused but valid binding). The module doc and the dto doc both advertise a set-secret path that never shipped. Delete `SetArgs` and `is_tauri_runtime`, fix both docs, replace the six one-field structs with `TenantArg`, then narrow the allow so `just web-clippy` reports leftovers. Overlaps F093 and F346.
- **F366 · Stale, redundant `#![allow(dead_code)]` / `unused_imports` in icon.rs, toast.rs and ui/mod.rs; two `IconName` variants (`More`, `ExternalLink`) are unused** — `apps/desktop/web-rs/src/components/icon.rs:6` · partially (the allows do not hide the variants: `components` is public API of a lib crate, so `dead_code` never fires on them). The toast.rs justification is no longer true and the ui/mod.rs allow is redundant. Delete the three allows and the stale comment; remove or annotate the two variants by hand.
- **F367 · Crate-wide `#![allow(private_interfaces)]` could be narrowed to the test-mountable modules** — `apps/desktop/web-rs/src/lib.rs:13` · partially (the cited `Route` example is not an instance of the lint, and the crate-wide allow is a documented decision with no real public consumer). At most move the allow onto `pub mod views;` and `pub mod components;`; skip the proposed cfg-doubled module tree.
- **F092 · `PermissionDescriptor` DTO is referenced nowhere** — `crates/azapptoolkit-dto/src/applications.rs:84` · confirmed. Dead, string-typed, superseded by `ResolvedPermission` plus `PermissionKind`. Delete it.
- **F089 · Dead Key Vault set-secret DTOs and binding arg struct for a command that no longer exists** — `crates/azapptoolkit-dto/src/keyvault.rs:83` · confirmed. `KvSetSecretInput`, `KvSecretMetadataDto`, their hand-written `Debug` redaction, half a redaction test and the `SetArgs` binding outlived the removed `kv_set_secret`. Delete all of them together and fix the stale `list / get / set secrets` header.
- **F090 · `ManagedIdentityBackup.azure_roles` / `azure_role_coverage` are never populated; backup-and-restore.md:89 and the DTO field doc claim an ARM scan that pass 3 deliberately skips** — `crates/azapptoolkit-dto/src/backup.rs:238` · partially (`azureRoleCoverage: null` already distinguishes never-scanned from scanned-empty, so the "no way to tell" claim is overstated). Every manifest writes empty roles and null coverage, and doc line 89 contradicts lines 115-116 and 207. Either remove the fields with `AzureRoleRef`/`AzureRoleCoverage`, or populate them from `list_managed_identity_azure_roles`; in both cases reword the field doc and align the architecture doc.
- **F159 · SelectedScopeLevel::key() has no callers and no test binding it to the serde spelling it claims to be** — `crates/azapptoolkit-core/src/scoping.rs:270` · confirmed. The doc promises the wire form equals `key()`, but serde's `rename_all` produces it independently and nothing asserts agreement. Add a round-trip test pinning `to_value(level) == json!(level.key())`, or delete `key()` and reword the doc.
- **F047 · `UserSettings::save` is still `pub`, bypasses the process-wide lock, and its stale doc is glued onto `mutate`** — `crates/azapptoolkit-core/src/settings.rs:136` · confirmed; also reported as F143. `save` is a byte-identical, unlocked twin of the private `save_locked`, called only by this file's tests, directly under the comment explaining why the write half is private. Delete it (tests use `mutate`) and split the doc block so `mutate` carries only its own text.
- **F119 · Dead code in the auth surface: `tenants()` is an async fn with no await and no callers; `EXCHANGE_SCOPES` is unused and hardcodes the commercial host while the scope catalog re-derives it** — `crates/azapptoolkit-auth/src/service/mod.rs:679` · confirmed; also reported as F211. `.tenants()` has zero callers and takes only a parking_lot lock, so its `async` is misleading; `EXCHANGE_SCOPES` is cited only by doc comments and is wrong for sovereign clouds. Delete `tenants()` (or drop `async`), reword the mod.rs:670 comment to name `tenant_context()`, and derive or remove `EXCHANGE_SCOPES`.
- **F176 · Root re-exports of targets::* are dead: callers import through the module path** — `crates/azapptoolkit-exchange/src/lib.rs:35` · confirmed. Twenty-two `targets` items are re-exported at the crate root, but every caller uses `azapptoolkit_exchange::targets::{...}`, and `verdict` and `references` have no root re-export at all. Drop the block, keep root re-exports for entry types only, and point mod.rs:43-46 at `aap::{...}`.
- **F198 · The permissions catalog carries no permission data, leaving `lookup_permission`, the catalog rung of the resolution ladder, and the permission entry types as dead code** — `crates/azapptoolkit-permissions/src/lib.rs:121` · confirmed. `catalog.json` holds six `{appId, displayName}` rows, so `lookup_permission` can only return `None`, the "1. Catalog" branch in `permissions_resolve.rs` is unreachable, and `list_catalog_resources` reports counts that are always 0. Rename the type to a resource directory and delete the entry types, the lookup and ladder step 1; keep the `CatalogResourceSummary` count fields, which other commands populate.
- **F086 · gallery partial_catalog is unreachable dead state in production** — `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands/gallery.rs:188` · confirmed. The only caller passes `false` and the comment says the corpus is never partial, yet the DTO field, the dialog's "only partly loaded" branch, fixtures and a test remain. Remove the parameter and field (a compile-checked wire change) and the dead branch.
- **F073 · AzureRolesResult.scanned is a constant alias of total; the DTO doc and the UI's "(capped)" branch are dead** — `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands/managed_identity.rs:217` · confirmed. `let scanned = total;` survived the cap's removal; the DTO doc still says "(capped for safety)" and the pane's "(capped)" message can never fire. Drop the field or redefine it as `total - skipped`, fix the doc and UI branch, and pin `scanned + skipped == total`.
- **F032 · Demo registers a fixture for `export_audit_csv`, a command that no longer exists on the IPC boundary** — `apps/desktop/web-rs/src/demo/mod.rs:665` · confirmed. The command was demoted to a `pub(crate)` helper, no binding calls it, and `demo_fixture_coverage.rs` checks only bindings to fixtures. Delete the fixture and the release-updater-demo.md:111 mention; optionally add the reverse assertion.
- **F065 · Orphaned doc comment in mail_scopes.rs describes is_org_wide_auth_row, which moved to azapptoolkit-exchange::verdict** — `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands/exchange/mail_scopes.rs:9` · confirmed. Lines 9-15 are the old doc of `is_org_wide_auth_row`, now glued onto `legacy_aap_scope`. Move the paragraph onto verdict.rs:29, which has no doc.
- **F064 · probe_candidate's doc comment is attached to struct ProbeOutcome, leaving the function undocumented and its too_many_arguments rationale orphaned** — `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands/permission_tester.rs:701` · confirmed. Plain `//` lines do not break a `///` run, so both paragraphs attach to `ProbeOutcome`. Move the struct and its own doc above, and put the fn doc plus the allow rationale on `probe_candidate`.
- **F131 · Five in-code docs have drifted from the code after the resource-aware refactors** — `crates/azapptoolkit-core/src/audit/scoring.rs:403` · confirmed. The SharePoint rule doc says "resource-stripped" while the code gates on resource, and `app_role_values` claims a scoring-pass role it no longer has. One doc names a nonexistent field, `is_scoped` lost its doc to `value_fully_scoped`, and the EWS comment sits above the wrong entry. Fix each as proposed; the verifier adds that "all nine additions" should read eight.
- **F175 · Stale history comments and broken intra-doc links in the crate's module docs** — `crates/azapptoolkit-exchange/src/client.rs:26` · confirmed. A "verify during the live transport spike" NOTE survives on a path with captured live traffic, three docs cite pre-split line counts, and three intra-doc links point at desktop-crate functions. Replace the NOTE with a one-line endpoint statement, trim the history, and turn the links into plain code spans.
- **F207 · Stale Key Vault crate comments: header claims no shared retry code while importing `core::http_retry`, `lib.rs` lists a nonexistent `delete_secret`, and `get_secret`'s `version` is unvalidated and never used by any caller** — `crates/azapptoolkit-keyvault/src/client.rs:3` · confirmed. The header contradicts the imports three lines below it, and `version` is spliced into the URL without the guard validate.rs mandates. Rewrite the header, fix lib.rs:10, drop or validate `version`, and drop the unused `Serialize` derive on `Paged<T>`.
- **F388 · Stale comments after the UriListEditor migration and a stale pane module header** — `apps/desktop/web-rs/src/views/enterprise_application_detail_pane/sso_tab.rs:534` · confirmed. "(one per line)" and "the textarea already shows" describe fields that are now `UriListEditor` rows, and mod.rs lists four of ten tabs. Reword the comments, point the header at `EnterpriseTab::ALL`, and collapse the duplicate `badge--danger` arms.
- **F383 · Wizard reset() and ClaimsEditorState::reset() rationale is stale, tool_dialog unmounts the dialog on close, so no instance is reused across opens** — `apps/desktop/web-rs/src/components/claims_editor.rs:266` · confirmed. `tool_dialog` renders nothing while closed, so each open is a fresh component and both `reset()` paths and their "reuse across opens" docs are redundant. Delete both, make the seed `Effect` a one-shot, and document the mount semantics as gallery_dialog.rs does.
- **F101 · Stale module headers and dead assertions in the invariants suite** — `apps/desktop/src-tauri/tests/repo_invariants/fanout.rs:193` · confirmed. `stale` is never pushed so its assertion cannot fire, `_handled_by_driver` is discarded, and two headers predate the `sources.rs` split. Delete the dead lines and update the headers.
- **F031 · FanOutTicker::tick's doc claims count and cap are read under one lock, they are not, and progress events can arrive out of order** — `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands/throttle.rs:216` · confirmed. The cap is read after `drop(guard)` and every caller emits outside any lock, so a progress bar can step backwards once. Replace the mutex with an `AtomicUsize`, fix the doc, and have `use_progress_stream` ignore a lower `done` for the same run.
- **F370 · Restored working set is not clamped to `MAX_OPEN_ITEMS`** — `apps/desktop/web-rs/src/state/open_items.rs:221` · confirmed. The snapshot is `set` as-is, and `open_item` evicts only one per push, so an oversized snapshot stays over cap indefinitely. Dedupe by `(kind, entity_id)` and trim by lowest `focused_at` while preserving dock order, with a 9-item test.
- **F348 · Permissions tab empty state points to the Entra portal and the hand-rolled kind filters can render an empty table silently** — `apps/desktop/web-rs/src/views/tabs/permissions_tab.rs:573` · confirmed. The copy predates the in-app "Grant access" button two lines above; the Application/Delegated toggles lack `aria-pressed` and both-off yields an empty table under a header. Reword the copy, add `aria-pressed` or a three-value `TabBar`, and render an empty-filter message.
- **F416 · view_smoke sits in the security shard, links four unrelated heavy subtrees, and double-links DisasterRecoveryView with shard 4** — `apps/desktop/web-rs/tests/gui_2.rs:10` · confirmed. `disaster_recovery_view_mounts` is superseded by dr.rs's real tests, and the shard header names a shell smoke that lives in gui_4. Delete the DR smoke, move the other three to gui_4 (or a new shard if size-bound), and fix the header.
- **F117 · Zeroize discipline is one-sided: the load path is meticulous, but the freshly issued refresh token, the raw /token body and the PKCE verifier are dropped un-wiped** — `crates/azapptoolkit-auth/src/service/mod.rs:455` · confirmed. `TokenResponse` fields are plain `String`, the full body sits in `Bytes`, and the `Zeroizing` copy of the verifier leaves the oauth2 original in place; only `access_token` is wiped via `AccessToken`. Type the response fields as `Zeroizing<String>`, wrap the body, drop or justify the verifier wrapper, and document which buffers are wiped.
- **F118 · Two keyring deletes run inline on the tokio runtime (sign_out and the InvalidGrant purge) and take the std `CHUNK_SET_LOCK` on a worker thread, contrary to the documented spawn_blocking rule** — `crates/azapptoolkit-auth/src/service/mod.rs:535` · confirmed. Save and load were moved to `spawn_blocking` for the same syscall loop, and the purge arm also holds the per-scope refresh lock. Add a `spawn_blocking` delete wrapper for both sites and consider a source-scan pin.
- **F229 · parking_lot and std::sync locks are mixed with no rule, and the std sites exist only to recover from poisoning parking_lot doesn't have** — `crates/azapptoolkit-auth/src/token_cache.rs:239` · confirmed. token_cache.rs uses both in one file, and settings.rs and throttle.rs carry poison-recovery prose that parking_lot makes moot. Pick one (parking_lot is already in the graph via tao), switch the four std sites, and write the rule into AGENTS.md.
- **F148 · CacheKind::idx is `self as usize` while the bucket array is sized by the hand-maintained ALL, a new variant compiles and panics with index-out-of-bounds on first use** — `crates/azapptoolkit-core/src/cache.rs:45` · confirmed. `ttl_for` forces a TTL arm but nothing forces `ALL` to grow, so a fifth variant panics inside a Tauri command. Make `idx()` an exhaustive match, add a const assert tying `ALL.len()` to the last discriminant, and a unit test over `ALL`.
- **F102 · `From<AuthError>` needs a wildcard arm because an internal crate's error enum is `#[non_exhaustive]`, so a new variant silently becomes `unknown_auth`** — `crates/azapptoolkit-dto/src/lib.rs:163` · confirmed. The workspace is `publish = false`, so the attribute only forces the `_ =>` arm, and the hand-listed pin cannot notice a new variant. Remove `#[non_exhaustive]` and the wildcard; token_adapter.rs's collapse can keep its wildcard by intent.
- **F189 · upsert_admin_oauth2_grant_in fabricates a Graph 500 for a client-side invariant instead of using Protocol** — `crates/azapptoolkit-graph/src/client/roles_grants.rs:342` · confirmed. A missing grant id surfaces as `graph_error` and reads like a Graph outage in logs and UI. Return `GraphError::Protocol(...)`; no caller matches on `Api { status: 500 }` here.
- **F217 · Transport helpers bound `B: Serialize + ?Sized` without `Sync`, so Send-ness of every write future depends on each call site** — `crates/azapptoolkit-graph/src/client/transport.rs:93` · confirmed. Eight helpers hold `&B` across an await, so a non-Sync body would fail opaquely inside `generate_handler!`. Add `+ Sync` to the eight bounds; no call site changes.
- **F305 · .gitignore carries Tauri-template Node/Vite entries for a toolchain the repo never had** — `.gitignore:43` · partially (the per-user agent-tool ignores are ordinary hygiene, not stale; only the Node/Vite block is a template leftover). Drop `node_modules/`, `.vite/` and `*.tsbuildinfo`, retitle the block for Trunk output, and at most reword the tool comments.
- **F331 · Dead CSS: the removed command palette and nine other unused class blocks remain in styles.css** — `apps/desktop/web-rs/styles.css:2126` · confirmed; also reported as F358 (partially: `.dr-view__secrets` and `.dr-view__secret-name` are live, only `.dr-view__secret-value` is dead). The `.cmd-palette*` block (with an `outline: none` and no focus-visible pair), `.mi-grant`, `.mi-scope-panel`, `.owner-list`, `.dash-links`, `.checkbox-list`, `.centered-pad`, `.ui-kbd`, `.sso-claims__row` and `.dr-view__secret-value` match nothing in src or tests. Delete them and add a selector-vs-source scan with an allow-list for `thaw-*` and `format!`-built modifiers.

### Duplication that a shared helper would absorb

The largest sub-theme. Most entries are a documented primitive or shared core that later code re-implemented by hand (`DetailLoadError`, `DirectorySearch`, `Badge`, `DataTable`, `UiError::not_found`, `core::thumbprint::canonical`, `collect_pages_from`). Behaviour then drifts between copies and each fix must land in several places.

#### F319 · medium · effort M · ~10 tab/section load failures bypass DetailLoadError: no Retry, and three of them print raw `error [code]: message`
`apps/desktop/web-rs/src/views/enterprise_application_detail_pane/access.rs:190` · confirmed; also reported as F379
**Problem.** frontend-workspace.md calls `DetailLoadError` the universal "message + Retry" block. Yet the Access tab (three sites), Permissions, App roles, Expose API, Authentication and Federated tabs render a bare `Body1.form-error` on load failure. A 429 or network blip on those tabs is a dead end until the item is reopened. The three Access sites also print the wire code inline:
```rust
<Body1 class="form-error">
    {format!("error [{}]: {}", e.code, e.message)}
</Body1>
```
The sign_in.rs comment cites a "detail-pane `error [code]: message` convention" that survives only in those three sites. The verifier notes that add_owner.rs and bulk_action_bar.rs are search-result strings rather than tab loads, and that readiness_view.rs already has a retry button.
**Proposal.** Replace each `Err(e) => <Body1 class="form-error">` in a Suspend load path with `<DetailLoadError error=e on_retry=... />`; every tab already owns a `reload` signal. Render sign_in.rs and readiness_view.rs message-first with the code muted, fix the stale comment, and add a repo_invariants scan rejecting `form-error` inside a `Suspend` `Err` arm.

#### F380 · medium · effort M · Both Owners tabs hand-roll the debounced user search that DirectorySearch provides (and the Access tab in the same pane already uses)
`apps/desktop/web-rs/src/views/enterprise_application_detail_pane/owners.rs:32` · confirmed
**Problem.** `DirectorySearch` is the one directory typeahead (debounce, 2-char gate, Suspense, exclusion set, per-row disabled state), and the Access tab two tabs over uses it. Both Owners tabs instead carry about 80 lines of the same machinery, a manual exclude of existing owners, a `<ul class="candidates">` and a different error class. Behaviour drifts between tabs of one pane and any fix lands in three places.
**Proposal.** Replace the query/candidates/Suspense block in `owners.rs` and `tabs/owners_tab.rs` with `<DirectorySearch scope=Users exclude=... query=raw_query clear_on_pick=false ... />`, keep the default-owners button and ConfirmDialog, and update GUI tests that reference `.candidates`.

#### F337 · medium · effort M · OwnersTab and AddOwnerButton hand-roll the directory search that DirectorySearch/OwnerPicker exist to replace, reintroducing the 'No matches under an untouched box' bug
`apps/desktop/web-rs/src/views/tabs/owners_tab.rs:457` · confirmed
**Problem.** owners_tab.rs and dialogs/add_owner.rs each carry a 300 ms debounce, a 2-char gate returning `Ok(Vec::new())`, and their own results markup. The Owners tab's Suspend checks only `filtered.is_empty()`, so an untouched box shows "No matches.", the exact bug `DirectorySearch` was extracted to end. Both files also duplicate the add-default-owners loop with different `existing` sources, and no backend `add_default_owners` command exists.
**Proposal.** Replace both search blocks with `OwnerPicker`/`DirectorySearch` (`exclude`, `action_label`, `row_disabled`, `clear_on_pick=false`); the Stage/Add label flips with `replacing`, so a `Signal` prop or two instances under `Show` is needed. Move the default-owners loop into one backend command or one shared frontend helper. Closely related to F380.

#### F320 · medium · effort M · Badge primitive bypassed at 45 sites (vs 20 uses) and one hand-written tone, badge--info, does not exist in styles.css
`apps/desktop/web-rs/src/views/enterprise_application_detail_pane/sso_tab.rs:231` · confirmed
**Problem.** badge.rs declares itself the one home for `.badge` chrome, yet 45 lines across 16 files hand-write `badge badge--...` against 20 `<Badge>` uses. That is how `badge--info` shipped without a rule:
```rust
sso::CertStatus::Staged => "badge badge--info",
```
A Staged SAML certificate, the state the rollover flow is about, renders with base chrome only; the verifier corrects the original "grey of unknown" claim. `Badge` also cannot express `--critical` or `--info`.
**Proposal.** Make `Badge.tone` an enum so an unknown tone is a compile error, add `.badge--info` to styles.css, migrate the 45 sites (mostly `(label, class)` tuples), and pin with a repo_invariants scan rejecting `"badge badge--` outside badge.rs.

#### F317 · medium · effort M · Eleven raw <table class="data-table"> instances bypass DataTable and get no keyboard row navigation
`apps/desktop/web-rs/src/views/tabs/permissions_tab.rs:580` · confirmed
**Problem.** `DataTable` exists so tables share the roving-tabindex Arrow/Home/End contract, yet 15 raw `<table class="data-table">` sites plus one `cert-rollover__table` exist against 7 `use_grid_keynav` callers. The App Registration detail tabs (Permissions, Credentials, Expose an API, App roles) are the most-used tables in the app and have no keynav. The shortcuts sheet's "Move between rows in a table" promise is therefore false there. The verifier excludes cache_diagnostics_dialog.rs:106, a headerless key/value table.
**Proposal.** Migrate the static-row tables to `DataTable`; for permissions_tab.rs:580 (keyed `<For>`) wire `use_grid_keynav` as findings.rs does, or add a keyed DataTable variant. Pin with a scan that a raw `data-table` outside data_table.rs sits in a file calling `use_grid_keynav`.

#### F182 · medium · effort S · Advanced-query flag is inconsistent between page 1 and continuations: collect_all_pages defaults to eventual, the SP index does the reverse
`crates/azapptoolkit-graph/src/client/transport.rs:175` · confirmed
**Problem.** `collect_all_pages` hardcodes the continuation flag:
```rust
self.collect_all_pages_with(page, true).await
```
Of its 12 callers only two issue page 1 with `ConsistencyLevel: eventual`. Ten issue page 1 without it and every later page with it. Pages 2+ then come from the eventually consistent store and can miss a just-written grant. The SP index inverts it (`$count=true` plus eventual on page 1, plain continuation), the half Microsoft's paging doc calls wrong. transport.rs:307-316 records this exact drift producing a silent wrong answer once, and no test asserts the page-2 header.
**Proposal.** Delete the defaulted wrapper (or default it to `false`) and pass the page-1 flag at every site. Drop `$count=true`/eventual from `list_service_principals_index`, or pass `true` to its capped continuation and document why. Add a wiremock regression asserting `header_is_missing("consistencylevel")` on the `$expand` page-2 mock.

- **F091 · `DirectoryIndexStatus` is a wire type defined twice outside the DTO crate, camelCase against the DTO convention** — `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands/applications/mod.rs:86` · confirmed; also reported as F019 (partially: the snake_case-convention claim is overstated, the dto crate has about 70 camelCase types; only location and duplication are deviations). The only IPC payload type outside `azapptoolkit-dto` is hand-mirrored in bindings/applications.rs with no compile check. Move it to `dto::applications`, `use` it from both sides, and fix the stale bindings/mod.rs header; keeping or dropping `rename_all` is a wire choice made on both sides together. See F419.
- **F419 · The one hand-written JSON demo fixture contradicts the 'typed fixtures cannot drift' guarantee** — `apps/desktop/web-rs/src/demo/mod.rs:381` · confirmed. `get_directory_index_status` is the only `json!` fixture because the frontend struct derives `Deserialize` only. Add `Serialize` and a typed builder, or move the struct into the DTO crate (F091).
- **F011 · create_application_core computes the initial secret lifetime without the 1..=730 clamp used by add_password** — `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands/applications/mod.rs:354` · partially (`GraphClient::add_password` keeps three other callers, so it is not deletable afterwards). Three copies of the 180-day default and one clamp; `Some(0)` or `Some(3650)` pass through, latent today because no caller sends the field. Extract `clamp_secret_lifetime_days` (or make `resolve_password_window` `pub(super)`) and route the core through it, with a table test for 0 and 9999.
- **F046 · Sign-out drops graph/exchange clients but never the kv/arm/la client maps; no helper makes the sweep exhaustive** — `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands/auth.rs:54` · partially (no document claims client-map exhaustiveness; "sweeps every kind" refers to cache kinds). No leak today, since the clients hold only an http pool and a token adapter, but `kv_clients` only grows. Add `AppState::forget_tenant` clearing all five maps and the inflight gates, and pin every `Mutex<HashMap<` field to it.
- **F096 · Backup manifest restates the `MiSubtype` and `CloudEnvironment` wire vocabularies as hand-written strings** — `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands/backup.rs:893` · confirmed. `mi_subtype_wire_label` repeats what serde's `rename_all = "camelCase"` emits, its test pins hand-typed strings, and `TenantBackup.cloud` round-trips `as_str()` as a `String` (the dto doc even lists the wrong labels). Type the fields as `MiSubtype` and `CloudEnvironment` with a serde repr matching today's labels; `CloudEnvironment` needs serde derives added first.
- **F171 · Desktop commands/exchange/tests.rs duplicates the crate's pure-function tests in the slowest-to-build crate** — `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands/exchange/tests.rs:81` · confirmed. About 14 of 29 desktop tests exercise crate-pure functions already pinned in targets.rs and verdict.rs, with a second fixture set that can drift. Delete the duplicates, keep the glue tests, and fold any extra assertion into the crate test first.
- **F203 · Role-definition name resolution is copy-pasted between the MI and Key Vault sweeps and cached per subscription-prefixed path; two high-privilege lists live outside `core::azure_roles`** — `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands/keyvault_rbac.rs:213` · partially (a tenant-level fetch path is unsafe for custom roles, so the cache key may use `role_id_tail` but the fetch must keep the first absolute path seen). The same 30-line block resolves `Owner` once per subscription. Extract `resolve_role_names`, key by GUID tail, and move both privilege lists into `core::azure_roles`.
- **F066 · permission_tester re-implements sharepoint_client_checked, map_sharepoint_err and held_orgwide_mail_grants instead of reusing them** — `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands/permission_tester.rs:1023` · confirmed. `sharepoint_test_err` is `sharepoint_item_err` by another name, the pre-acquire inlines what `sharepoint_client_checked` claims to own, and `orgwide_mailbox_grant` repeats the `held_orgwide_mail_grants` pipeline. Make the sharepoint.rs helpers `pub(crate)` and route the tester through them; unify the mail helper alongside its tri-state change.
- **F013 · PermissionKind -> manifest entry-type mapping is written three times in permissions.rs, one copy being the helper extracted to avoid that** — `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands/permissions.rs:759` · confirmed. `declare_app_permission` re-inlines `entry_type_for` with identical code and message. Use `entry_type_for(kind)?` there and `entry_type_for(kind).ok()` in `remove_declared_permission`.
- **F062 · The org-wide strip loop and the declare-then-grant-appRole step are duplicated across the SharePoint paths and against the Exchange core instead of being shared helpers** — `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands/sharepoint.rs:366` · confirmed. - **F083 · Inconsistent not-found wire codes: two SSO sites emit a bare `not_found`, and three other sites double the suffix via the factory** — `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands/sso/mod.rs:471` · partially (the drift is broader than the SSO pair: three exchange sites pass an already-suffixed resource, yielding `*_not_found_not_found`). - **F081 · set_sso_mode / get_sso_summary are stringly typed and an unrecognised mode silently disables SSO** — `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands/sso/mod.rs:646` · confirmed. `mode.as_str()` maps anything but "saml"/"oidc" to `Null`, so a casing slip is a destructive PATCH, and the frontend re-derives the same literals. Add a serde `SsoMode` enum and a tagged `SsoSummary` enum in `dto/sso.rs`, matching `CertStatus`/`RolloverPhase`.
- **F095 · Closed-set wire fields typed as `String` where the crate already uses serde enums elsewhere** — `crates/azapptoolkit-dto/src/permission_tester.rs:19` · confirmed. Introduce enums with matching `rename_all` and `#[serde(other)]` where the set may grow, starting with the `verdict` and `status` fields.
- **F172 · AccessRight/ScopeType are stringly typed; the two RestrictAccess comparisons already disagree on trimming** — `crates/azapptoolkit-exchange/src/models.rs:96` · partially (both sites are already pinned case-insensitive and the cited "this one did not" note concerns AppId; the real drift is that aap.rs trims and verdict.rs does not). - **F103 · Timestamps cross IPC as `DateTime<Utc>` in some DTOs and RFC3339 `String` in others, with no documented convention on either side** — `crates/azapptoolkit-dto/src/sso.rs:360` · partially (the String fields are tolerated per field through `util::time_ago`, whereas `DateTime<Utc>` fails a whole payload on one odd Graph stamp; the defect is the undocumented split). - **F084 · active_thumbprint (and the board's Thumbprint column) bypass core::thumbprint::canonical** — `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands/sso/mod.rs:1616` · confirmed. `build_rollover` stores the raw `preferredTokenSigningKeyThumbprint`, which can differ in case, so the board and the SSO tab can print two spellings of one certificate. Add `resolve_pattern(pattern, default_prefix, app_id)` and make the three methods one-liners.

The `NotFound => Ok(None)` match appears in five methods plus a batched variant. Add `get_json_optional<T>` to transport.rs and optionally fold the two raw readers behind thin wrappers.

The sign-in-activity report and `list_all_sites` re-implement the origin guard and page cap, and `const MAX_PAGES` is declared five times. Route the report through `collect_pages_from`, add a capped variant for sites, and hoist `MAX_PAGES`.
- **F201 · Key Vault and ARM transports duplicate the same status→variant mapping closure; a shared generic transport is now feasible because both enums come from `http_error_enum!`** — `crates/azapptoolkit-keyvault/src/client.rs:147` · partially (core's http_retry.rs documents leaving per-attempt status mapping per crate, and all three `retry_class_for` bodies are identical, so there is no divergence today). The two transports are near byte-identical. If pursued, emit a constructor trait from the macro and add a `core::http_transport::send_with_retry<E>`; keep Graph's transport separate.

### Oversized modules and proposed splits

All low. Three command files have outgrown one concern (audit.rs, permissions.rs, sso/mod.rs), and the repo already proved the fix twice: `applications/` and `commands/exchange/` are directory modules with flat re-exports that keep `generate_handler!` paths stable.

- **F028 · Split commands/audit.rs (2011 lines) into a directory module by concern** — `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands/audit.rs:854` · confirmed. Run orchestration, six prefetchers, per-principal scoring, the cache entry and three export writers share one file; `commands/exchange/` already proves the directory shape. Create `audit/{mod,prefetch,score,cache,export,tests}.rs` with re-exports so `generate_handler!` paths hold; keep `run_audit` and `classify_audit_failure` together for the fanout.rs per-module pin.
- **F016 · permissions.rs is 1417 lines spanning six concerns; applications/ was already split the same way** — `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands/permissions.rs:13` · confirmed. Six section banners mark catalog, manifest, consent, grant, downgrade and revoke, and the glob re-export pattern in applications/mod.rs is proven. Split into `permissions/{catalog,manifest,consent,grant,downgrade,revoke}.rs`, ideally alongside the F013 rework.
- **F076 · Split sso/mod.rs (2349 lines) by concern: create, validate, config, rollover, board, metadata** — `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands/sso/mod.rs:1` · confirmed. The largest file under commands/ holds six concerns plus about 566 lines of tests; `claims.rs` and `gallery.rs` were extracted on the same rationale. Split as listed with flat re-exports; only docs/architecture/auth-and-consent.md:159 needs updating, since the `.claude/rules` glob already covers the subdir.
- **F387 · sso_tab.rs packs a 370-line Suspend closure with five nested command closures; Overview's visibility toggle hand-rolls spawn_local unlike its two siblings** — `apps/desktop/web-rs/src/views/enterprise_application_detail_pane/sso_tab.rs:121` · partially (the hand-rolled toggle does not skip re-auth handling; it reaches the same `report_command_error` sink, so the difference is stylistic). The rollover panel keeps row rendering, phase guidance and five mutations inside one async closure. Split into `sso_tab/{mod,rollover_panel,rollover_row,phase_callout}.rs`, migrate the toggle to `use_command().run_toast_err`, and keep `pub mod sso_tab` for the GUI test.
- **F063 · Two internal helpers (migrate_one, probe_candidate) could take context structs; converting the three Tauri commands to request structs is a wire-format change, not a drop-in** — `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands/exchange/aap_migration.rs:148` · partially (Tauri 2 deserialises each parameter by name, so a struct-arg command needs the frontend to nest the payload; three more `too_many_arguments` allows exist than the finding lists). Introduce `MigrationContext` and `ProbeContext` for the two internal helpers and drop their allows; leave the three command signatures flat unless bindings change in the same PR.

### Performance

Seven of the bucket's fourteen medium findings sit here. They are not hot loops but avoidable network work: bypassed single-flight gates, over-broad cache invalidation, repeated Graph chains, an unretried token endpoint and a cache attached to the wrong read.

#### F007 · medium · effort S · list_applications_with_pairing bypasses the SP-index single-flight gate and has no gate of its own; two frontend surfaces call it
`apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands/applications/mod.rs:186` · confirmed
