- **F357 · List refetch after a mutation remounts the whole `LoadedApps` subtree (Suspense), losing scroll position** — `apps/desktop/web-rs/src/components/virtual_list.rs:132` · partially (the snap-to-top effect is not the mechanism: `<Suspense>` plus `Suspend` in application_list.rs re-creates `LoadedApps` and its `VirtualList` on every refetch, so the proposed `reset_key` prop would not help). After a delete, remove-expired or Fix bumps `apps_reload`, an operator triaging row 3,800 is thrown back to row 0. Switch the body to `<Transition>` feeding `LoadedApps` a signal of rows and clamp instead of snapping, or lift the scroll offset into a view-owned signal restored on mount; amend the :132 comment.
- **F355 · Toast stack neither dedupes identical recovery toasts nor protects sticky ones from the drop-oldest cap** — `apps/desktop/web-rs/src/state/toasts.rs:28` · confirmed (the burst scenario is overstated since list resources fail into `DetailLoadError` and the bulk bar stops on the first fatal code, so N identical toasts needs N operator actions). `push_toast` has no dedupe and `drain(0..overflow)` evicts by position, so the cap can drop a sticky Re-authenticate toast while transient successes survive. Return the existing id for an identical kind, message and action label, evict non-sticky toasts first, and add two host tests.
- **F322 · ~90 hard-coded '(s)' plurals in operator-facing strings plus ~14 inline `if n == 1` copies, while util::plural is private** — `apps/desktop/web-rs/src/util.rs:181` · partially (the raw count of 120 includes Rust patterns like `Ok(s)`; about 91 operator-facing literals remain, and the bulk-bar assertions are host-side unit tests). Toasts, confirm bodies and DR reports read "app(s)" while fourteen sites re-implement the three-line helper. Make `util::plural` pub, add `count_noun(n, one, many)`, migrate the inline branches first and then the literals, updating the in-file bulk_action_bar tests.
- **F321 · Expiry and date formatting is inconsistent: three phrasings for 'days left', six copies of date-only formatting, one hard-coded plural ('1 days left')** — `apps/desktop/web-rs/src/views/enterprise_application_detail_pane/sso_tab.rs:247` · confirmed. The Credentials tab says "7d left", the SSO tab "7 days left" or "1 days left", the list row "3 days ago", and six sites copy `d.date_naive().to_string()` while three repeat "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M UTC". Add `fmt_date`, `fmt_datetime` and `expiry_label(days)` to util.rs with host-side tests, replace the sites and delete `enterprise_application_detail_pane/mod.rs::fmt_date`.
- **F327 · Eight row action buttons ignore the documented `cell-mid` rule and sit ~7px below their row text** — `apps/desktop/web-rs/src/views/credentials_dashboard.rs:110` · confirmed. `.data-table td` is `vertical-align: top`, so the Open buttons in credentials_dashboard, sso_certificates_dashboard, consent_grants_view, app_permission_grants_view, key_vault_view, owners_tab, expose_api_tab and sharepoint_sites_section ride low against two-line identity cells; 22 other sites use the class. Add `class="cell-mid"` to the eight `<td>`s, and longer term let DataTable/AuditDashboard render the action cell through an `actions` prop.
- **F325 · Settings form and a handful of sub-section regions use Spinner/plain text as the Suspense fallback instead of the documented skeletons** — `apps/desktop/web-rs/src/views/settings_view.rs:73` · partially (the Tiny sub-section spinners and the gallery "Searching…" are closer to the doc's inline busy state, and 23 skeleton sites exist, not 27). The whole Settings form falls back to a Medium Spinner, and permission_picker.rs:221, managed_scope_group_panel.rs:254 and cache_diagnostics_dialog.rs:103 use plain "Loading…" text. Swap Settings to `<DetailSkeleton />`, the text fallbacks to `<SkeletonList rows=N />`, and judge the Tiny spinners case by case.
- **F326 · Empty states use four renderings (bare Body1, .muted <p>, .hint Body1, EmptyState) on adjacent surfaces** — `apps/desktop/web-rs/src/views/tabs/credentials_tab.rs:661` · partially (`.hint` also sets a smaller font size, so `.hint` and `.muted` are different jobs and should not be merged). "No matches." ships as `<p class="muted">`, `<Body1>` and via `EmptyState` across the owner picker, owners tab and directory search, and bare `<Body1>` empties in credentials_tab, expose_api_tab and app_roles predate the primitives. Route table-level empties through DataTable's `empty_message` and section-level ones through `EmptyState`, and document `.muted` as colour-only body copy versus `.hint` as the smaller field hint.

### Graph API capabilities not yet adopted

#### F161 · medium · effort M · disabledByMicrosoftStatus is not modeled on Application/ServicePrincipal; apps Microsoft has disabled for policy violation are invisible to the audit
`crates/azapptoolkit-core/src/models.rs:28` · confirmed
**Problem.** `Application` (models.rs:26-75) and `ServicePrincipal` (:127-173) have no `disabled_by_microsoft_status`, the application and SP `$select` lists omit `disabledByMicrosoftStatus`, and a repo grep finds nothing. Graph v1.0 exposes the property on both objects (`DisabledDueToViolationOfServicesAgreement` for suspicious, abusive or malicious activity), which is Microsoft's own "this app is malicious" flag and also explains "the integration silently stopped working" tickets. The audit already reads adjacent posture fields (`verifiedPublisher`, `servicePrincipalLockConfiguration`, `isFallbackPublicClient`) from the same `$select`.
**Proposal.** Add `#[serde(default)] pub disabled_by_microsoft_status: Option<String>` to both models (additive, IPC-compatible), append the property to the `$select` lists, add a high-severity rule "Disabled by Microsoft: {status}" with a table test, a badge on the app and SP headers, and a CHANGELOG entry for the ranking change.

#### F078 · medium · effort M · Conditional Access view ignores workload-identity targeting (conditions.clientApplications)
`apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands/conditional_access.rs:57` · partially (the finding's "pass the SP object id in, the detail pane has it" is wrong: the CA tab lives on the app-registration pane keyed only by appId, so the resolution belongs in the backend)
**Problem.** `CaConditions` deserialises only `applications`, so the `clientApplications { includeServicePrincipals, excludeServicePrincipals, servicePrincipalFilter }` axis of workload-identity Conditional Access is never read, and `to_dto` returns `None` when `applications` is absent. A workload-identity policy with the usual `includeApplications=["All"]` is reported as "applies (all)" for every app in the tenant, and a policy that blocks THIS app's SP is invisible when its `applications` condition names other resources: mis-reported in both directions.
```
let apps = policy.conditions.as_ref().and_then(|c| c.applications.as_ref())?;
let reason = applies_reason(apps, app_id)?;
```
**Proposal.** Add `client_applications: Option<CaClientApplications>` to `CaConditions`; resolve the SP object id in `list_conditional_access_for_app` via `get_service_principal_by_app_id`, skipping the client-side check when no SP exists. In `applies_reason` apply exclude-wins on `excludeServicePrincipals`, then require an SP match when `clientApplications` is present (`workloadIdentity` / `workloadIdentityAll` / `workloadIdentityFilter` codes) before the resource-side reasons; extend the table tests, document the codes on the DTO and update the tab copy. F268 reports the same gap from the model side.

#### F268 · medium · effort M · Conditional Access tab ignores `conditions.clientApplications`, so workload-identity CA policies targeting the service principal are never shown
`crates/azapptoolkit-core/src/models.rs:614` · partially (the claim that the panel lives only on the app-registration pane is false: `ConditionalAccessPanel` is already mounted on the enterprise pane at panels.rs:133, but it takes only `app_id`, so the SP object id is never passed; workload-identity CA is also Workload Identities Premium-gated)
**Problem.** `pub struct CaConditions { #[serde(default)] pub applications: Option<CaApplications> }` is the whole condition model. For a tool whose subject is the service principal, a policy that blocks this SP from untrusted locations or ties it to SP risk renders as "no policies apply", which actively misleads an AADSTS53003 troubleshooting session. The fetch returns whole policy objects with no `$select` (directory.rs:78-95), so this is a model and classifier change with no new Graph call or scope.
**Proposal.** Add `client_applications` (include/exclude SP ids plus `servicePrincipalFilter {mode, rule}`), extend `to_dto`/`applies_reason` to take the SP object id with exclude-wins semantics and distinct reason codes, add a target-axis field to `ConditionalAccessPolicyDto`, and widen the panel prop from `app_id` to `(app_id, sp_object_id)` so the enterprise-pane instance passes it. Table tests mirror `all_matches_unless_excluded`; adjust the README:130 wording.

- **F264 · Adopt servicePrincipalRiskDetections (v1.0) to flag risky workload identities in the audit** — `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands/audit.rs:1287` · confirmed · also reported as F276 (`audit.rs:1312`, confirmed, proposing `/identityProtection/riskyServicePrincipals` for the same gap). The SP-only scoring path folds in only sign-in and legacy-policy signals, while Entra ID Protection's leaked-credential and malicious-application detections (v1.0, Workload ID Premium, not available in China) go unread. Add an on-demand `IdentityRiskEvent.Read.All` / `IdentityRiskyServicePrincipal.Read.All` scope feature with a catalog row, prefetch best-effort beside `prefetch_sign_in_activity`, add `risk_state`/`risk_level` to `AuditItem` additively and a Rule 19 with a `RemediationKind::DisableSignIn` pointer, a Findings facet and an Enterprise overview chip; consent must be requested from readiness or settings (no Grant consent on audit surfaces), a failed read must mark the run degraded, and a CHANGELOG entry covers the ranking shift.
- **F259 · Adopt appCredentialSignInActivities (same AuditLog.Read.All token) for per-credential last-used and an unused-credential finding** — `crates/azapptoolkit-graph/src/client/directory.rs:291` · confirmed. The only sign-in signal is per-app `last_sign_in` from the beta SP report, while the beta `appCredentialSignInActivities` report rides the same token and `audit_reports` roles and returns `keyId`, `keyType`, `credentialOrigin` and `lastSignInDateTime`. Add `list_app_credential_sign_in_activities()` with the same cache bucket, page cap and origin guards, join on `keyId` into the credential DTOs, add a "credential unused for N days" rule gated on `sign_in_report_available`, and show last-used in the Credentials tab; note Global-cloud-only in the capability description.
- **F260 · Read tenant/app management policies (v1.0) to pre-validate secret lifetimes and explain policy-driven 400s** — `crates/azapptoolkit-graph/src/client/credentials.rs:32` · confirmed. `add_password` posts whatever lifetime the caller chose, so tenant `passwordAddition` and `passwordLifetime` restrictions surface only as an opaque Graph 400 after the operator picked a lifetime. Add `get_default_app_management_policy()` and `list_app_management_policies_for(app_id)` on the existing `policy_token` (degrading to None like CA), warn or clamp in the add-secret dialog, map the policy-violation error code, and add a posture card when the tenant default is disabled. F270 is the audit-side view of the same gap.
- **F270 · No visibility of tenant app-management policies (credential lifetime / type enforcement); audit and Credentials tab cannot explain a policy-rejected secret add** — `crates/azapptoolkit-core/src/audit/permissions.rs:29` · partially (`LONG_LIVED_SECRET_DAYS = 365` is a deliberate legacy port citing Credential-Analysis.ps1:169, not a defect, and nothing currently misleads; replacing it would be a CHANGELOG-gated ranking change). Rules 7 and 17 and the Credentials tab give no hint whether the tenant already enforces a policy, whether this app is exempted, or why an add just failed. Phase 1 (read-only): the three `GraphClient` reads and a best-effort `TenantAppPolicyDto` in the audit prefetch, keeping the 365-day floor and adding the effective `passwordLifetime` as an additional advisory comparison, plus a Credentials-tab Callout, a Home posture line and a `capabilities.rs` entry; Phase 2 writes behind `policy_write`.
- **F265 · SAML SSO does not expose `requestSignatureVerification` (signed AuthnRequest enforcement), a v1.0 application property** — `crates/azapptoolkit-graph/src/client/applications.rs:108` · confirmed. `get_application_sso_fields` selects only `id,appId,identifierUris,web,spa` and `ApplicationSsoPatch` has no field for `isSignedRequestRequired` or `allowedWeakAlgorithms`, so the one SAML hardening control the tab cannot show or set is signed-request enforcement. Add the property to the `$select` and the patch struct, render a toggle plus the weak-algorithm allowance in the SAML summary, and consider a low-weight rule for apps allowing `rsaSha1` with a CHANGELOG note.
- **F274 · Add a tenant-consent-settings posture tile (user consent policy, admin consent workflow, pending consent requests)** — `crates/azapptoolkit-graph/src/client/policies.rs:1` · confirmed. policies.rs is claims-mapping only, and no read of `authorizationPolicy`, `adminConsentRequestPolicy`, `permissionGrantPolicies` or `appConsentRequests` exists, so the Delegated-grants audit inventories consents without the tenant setting that produces them. Add the three reads behind `policy_token()` (no new scope), a `TenantConsentPostureDto`, and a best-effort `check_consent_posture` rendered in the Home Security Posture card and the Delegated-grants header with a Callout when user consent is unrestricted; pending requests as a second step behind a new `consent_requests` feature.

### Product-gap proposals

#### F266 · medium · effort M · Bulk-delete confirmation says 'cannot be undone' although Entra soft-deletes for 30 days; no in-app 'Recently deleted' list or restore
`apps/desktop/web-rs/src/components/bulk_action_bar.rs:817` · partially (the single-app dialog at application_detail_pane.rs:269 already says deletion can be undone within 30 days, so only the bulk bar misinforms; the missing recycle-bin surface is a genuine absence)
**Problem.** `delete_application` is a plain DELETE that Entra soft-deletes for 30 days, yet no `deletedItems` code exists anywhere and the bulk completion toast has no undo path. The bulk bar, seeded by multi-select and "Fix all N" flows, tells the operator the action is irreversible at the one moment they most need the true message: after a mis-targeted bulk delete they reach for `Restore-MgDirectoryDeletedItem` or recreate the app, minting a new appId and breaking every consumer. The list read needs only `Directory.Read.All` (the sign-in scope); restore and purge ride the existing `write` feature, and `invalidate_app_lists` plus the pairing refresh is the only cache work.
```
{move || format!("Permanently delete the {} selected app registration(s). This cannot be undone.", n())}
```
**Proposal.** (S) Reword bulk_action_bar.rs:817 to match the single-app dialog. (M) Add `commands/applications/deleted.rs` with list, restore and purge over `/directory/deletedItems/microsoft.graph.application` (paged with `$top`), registered in `generate_handler![]` with bindings, a "Recently deleted" surface with restore, `invalidate_app_lists` on Ok, `demo::register_fixtures` entries, and an "Undo (restore N)" action on the bulk-delete toast.

#### F267 · medium · effort L · Build the file-bridged posture snapshot + drift report (#109); every seam it names exists and is unblocked
`crates/azapptoolkit-dto/src/consent.rs:9` · confirmed
**Problem.** The owner's 2026-08-04 comment on issue #109 retired the blocker and wrote the design: `PostureSnapshot` = cached `AuditItem`s + `AppPermissionGrantDto`s + coverage + `sign_in_report_available`, a pure `audit::drift::diff`, five commands and a Drift sub-tab. Nothing has landed, although every precondition holds: `save_backup_to_file`/`load_backup_from_file`, `list_app_role_assigned_to`, serde-defaulted `AuditItem` fields. The two hazards the design calls out remain: `list_app_permission_grants` degrades a failed resource to `Vec::new()` (a naive diff would print "every Exchange grant removed"), and `matches_finding` is frontend-only, so a backend diff cannot bucket by stable marker. `AppPermissionGrantDto` still lacks the `resource_app_id` diff key. For an admin this is the difference between "today's posture" and "what changed since the last attestation", the question audit committees ask.
**Proposal.** Implement the owner's design: (a) additive `resource_app_id: Option<String>` and a per-resource `grant_coverage` record in place of the silent `Vec::new()`; (b) move `matches_finding` to `azapptoolkit_core::audit::classify_finding` with filter.rs delegating; (c) `dto/snapshot.rs` and a pure `core::audit::drift::diff` with fail-closed rules (suppress removals for uncovered resources, omit the unused dimension unless both sides have the report, refuse tenant/cloud mismatch, newer schema and cancelled baselines); (d) `commands/snapshot.rs` riding `save_export_via_dialog`; (e) a read-only Drift sub-tab in `SecurityView` with the baseline on `TenantScopedUi` plus `reset()` and the pinning assertion. No new scopes, Graph calls or persistence policy.

#### F278 · medium · effort M · Audit never reads federated identity credentials, so a planted or over-broad OIDC trust is invisible in the security workbench
`crates/azapptoolkit-core/src/federation.rs:3` · confirmed
**Problem.** federation.rs:3-7 explains that a FIC is a trust "with no secret to leak and no expiry to notice", yet `grep -ni federated commands/audit.rs` returns nothing and `score_application` takes only `&Application`. The audit scores secrets and certificates (Rules 5-9, 17) and nudges toward federation, so an attacker-planted trust (an `iss` from a GitHub org the tenant does not own, or a flexible `claimsMatchingExpression` matching every branch) or a FIC left after a repo was archived produces a clean row. `batch_list_federated_credentials` already exists for the backup path, `validate_federated_credential` gives a classifier, and fixtures exist in `client/tests/credentials.rs`.
**Proposal.** In `run_audit`, fetch FICs per app through the backup's batched reader (bounded to apps with app permissions or admin-consented scopes), pass `federated: Vec<FederatedIdentityCredential>` into `score_application`, and add advisory Rule 19: list issuers and subjects, flag `claimsMatchingExpression`, issuers outside a per-tenant allowlist in `TenantDefaults` (added to the exhaustive `apply_tenant_defaults` destructure), and `sub` values without a ref or environment constraint. New `issue::FEDERATED_TRUST_REVIEW` marker and finding group with no one-click fix, CSV/JSON export columns, table tests.

- **F269 · Enterprise-app (SP-only) audit rows skip the ownership rule even though SP owners live in this tenant** — `crates/azapptoolkit-core/src/audit/scoring.rs:1045` · partially (the cited `findings.rs:231` invariant does not exist, the real pins are scoring/tests.rs:775 and the arch doc; first-party Microsoft SPs must be excluded on `appOwnerOrganizationId` or the group becomes noise; and the ownership rule carries no points, so this is a finding-group change, not a ranking shift). SP owners are a local property the detail pane reads and writes, yet `score_service_principal` passes `None` for owner_count with the note "SP owners aren't audited", so ownerless gallery and foreign apps never reach the ownership group or the campaign. Add `owner_count: Option<usize>` to `SpAuditInput`, resolve it via `$batch` in `sp_audit_candidates`, run Rule 14 for SP rows, and route `AddOwner` through a new `bulk_add_sp_owner` core wrapping `add_enterprise_app_owner` (never `bulk_add_owner`); table tests for 0/1/2 owners and unresolved `None`.
- **F272 · Switching tenants requires a relaunch by design; ergonomics of the switch could be improved without breaking the single-tenant invariant** — `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands/config.rs:27` · partially (single-tenant-per-process is a documented invariant in backup-and-restore.md:10-13 that the whole DR shape and the cross-tenant cache defence rest on, so in-process switching must be raised as an architecture decision, not shipped as a feature). `set_auth_config` persists one client/tenant pair and relaunches while everything below the auth service is already per-tenant; the 0.30.0 CHANGELOG names the consultant persona who pays a restart and a browser round trip per switch. Invariant-preserving form: saved profiles (`label, client_id, tenant_id`) in settings.json and a "Switch tenant…" picker in the account menu that saves the chosen pair and relaunches via `set_auth_config` + `restart_app`, preserving the last export path.
- **F273 · Credential-expiry dashboard covers app registrations only; credentials held directly on service principals are invisible tenant-wide** — `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands/credentials.rs:58` · partially (README:61-62 honestly says "every app registration's", and widening the pinned, capped, shared SP index would inflate every consumer's payload, so a separate `$select` scan or `$batch` is the right shape; SP-held credentials outside SAML certs are uncommon). SP `passwordCredentials`/`keyCredentials` render on the per-SP pane but appear in no tenant-wide list, Home card or audit rule. Add `principal_kind` and `service_principal_id` to `CredentialRowDto`, a dedicated `/servicePrincipals` scan skipping SAML signing certs and SPs paired to a local app, route Open by kind through `session.open_item`, a Kind chip, CSV header and README wording.
- **F275 · SCIM provisioning is read-only: no start / pause / restart (clear quarantine) for a job the pane already diagnoses** — `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands/enterprise_application.rs:335` · confirmed. `get_enterprise_app_provisioning` is the only synchronization command and the panel shows the quarantine reason with no buttons, while Graph exposes `POST .../synchronization/jobs/{jobId}/start|pause|restart` under `Synchronization.ReadWrite.All` and the `sync` feature and token plumbing already exist. Add a `sync_write` feature, `start`/`pause`/`restart_synchronization_job(reset_scope)` beside `list_synchronization_jobs`, three commands with bindings, and confirm-gated buttons carrying the quarantine reason as the dialog subject.
- **F277 · No headless/CLI mode for scheduled posture reports; audit orchestration is coupled to Tauri** — `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands/audit.rs:127` · partially (the auth crate has no device-code flow and loopback PKCE needs a browser, so headless on a jump host is XL not L; the README promises no scheduled reporting; and the per-call-site cancel/fanout invariants must survive extraction). No library crate depends on Tauri, but `run_audit`, `prefetch_*`, `score_sp_only`, the exports, `list_credential_expirations` and `backup_tenant` take `State`/`AppHandle`, so "email me the weekly posture" sends admins back to PowerShell. Step 1: extract the orchestration into a Tauri-free module behind a `ProgressSink` trait plus `CancelToken`, leaving the command as a thin adapter with the pins on it; step 2, separate: an `azapptoolkit-cli` bin only after device-code auth and headless keyring behaviour are decided, kept out of the release matrix until gated.
- **F279 · Observed-activity panel promises a 'GETs-only → Downgrade' verdict but renders raw rows with no method roll-up (a gate-safe partial of #110)** — `apps/desktop/web-rs/src/views/tabs/usage_panel.rs:5` · confirmed. Both headers promise the "only GETs observed" reading, the summary is a bare count over up to 200 rows, and `GraphUsageResult` has no method totals although the KQL already summarises by `RequestMethod, Path`; a method-only roll-up is factual and stays inside #110's gate on the path-family map. Add `method_totals` (or a second `summarize by RequestMethod` in the same query, since rows are capped), render "Write methods observed: none" or "POST 12, PATCH 3" with an info Callout saying this is not a permission verdict, and link Downgrade only when zero non-GET calls were seen and `truncated == false`.
- **F280 · Credential-expiry dashboard has no owner data, so an 'expiring in 7 days' row cannot be turned into a notification** — `crates/azapptoolkit-dto/src/credentials.rs:13` · confirmed. `CredentialRowDto` carries no owners, so the next admin step, telling the owner to rotate, means opening each app's Owners tab; the audit already `$expand`s owners on the same `/applications` scan (20-owner truncation noted). Add `owner_emails: Vec<String>`, expand owners in `list_credential_expirations`, an Owners column with a pre-filled `mailto:` "Notify…" and a "Copy owner emails" action on the expiring-soon banner, owners in the CSV, ownerless rows marked to route to AddOwner; no new scope.
- **F283 · Bulk create accepts only a pasted JSON array of three fields: no file picker, no CSV, no owners/permissions columns to seed a migration from a legacy inventory** — `crates/azapptoolkit-dto/src/bulk.rs:115` · partially (the page already takes a pasted JSON array with a `validate_only` dry-run, so "retype three fields at a time" is false; the file picker, CSV shape and extra columns are genuinely absent). `load_backup_from_file` is the app's only `blocking_pick_file` caller and reads the toolkit's own shape. Add `load_bulk_create_specs_from_file` (CSV `DisplayName,SignInAudience,Description,Owners,Permissions` and JSON) beside it feeding the existing textarea and validate path, extend `BulkCreateSpec` additively with `owner_upns`/`permissions` wired to `bulk_add_owner`/`bulk_grant_permissions` after create, and document the column contract in docs/DEVELOPMENT.md.

### Docs, demo, packaging and release tooling

#### F247 · medium · effort S · No network/proxy/TLS guidance for enterprise deployments; Requirements omit the updater hosts
`README.md:266` · confirmed
**Problem.** README.md:292-298 lists the Microsoft hosts but not github.com, although tauri.conf.json:47-48 sets the updater endpoint there (assets redirect to objects.githubusercontent.com), and no doc mentions proxy, firewall, allowlist or TLS inspection. The shipped binary honours `HTTPS_PROXY`/`NO_PROXY` and the Windows static proxy (reqwest `auto_sys_proxy`, hyper-util reads ProxyEnable/ProxyServer, no PAC), trusts the OS store via rustls-platform-verifier, and service/mod.rs:183-192 already classifies the TLS-intercepting-proxy sign-in error, none of which an MDM or Group Policy rollout can learn from the docs. The verifier drops the NO_PROXY/loopback note as moot: the loopback redirect is an inbound browser-to-listener hop.
**Proposal.** Add a "Network requirements" subsection under Requirements: the full per-feature host allowlist plus the two updater hosts; a "Behind a proxy" note (env vars, OS static proxy, no PAC/WPAD); a "TLS inspection" note (OS trust store, no certificate pinning); a troubleshooting bullet for the text/html-from-login-host error; and the per-cloud sovereign hosts already in the CSP (tauri.conf.json:25).

#### F413 · medium · effort S · Demo credential and certificate dates are fixed and have aged past today, so list badges contradict the tabs they open
`apps/desktop/web-rs/src/demo/mod.rs:116` · confirmed
**Problem.** Every showcase secret (2025-08-30, 2025-07-09, 2025-09-28, 2024-02-01) and all filler secrets (2026-01-31) are past as of 2026-09-26, and `cred_status` is hard-coded per DemoApp and copied onto the row, so the list says Expiring or Active while credentials_tab.rs computes Expired from `end_date_time` against `Utc::now()`. The SSO board pairs an expiry of 2026-08-27 with "15 days". The author already used relative dates for `completed_at` (fixtures.rs:672-675, the finding's :562-565 citation was mislocated), so the pattern exists.
```
f::password_credential("crm-prod-secret", "Hq9", f::date(2025, 8, 30)),
f::password_credential("crm-legacy-secret", "a1Z", f::date(2024, 2, 1)),
```
**Proposal.** Replace the fixed `f::date(..)` values with offsets from `Utc::now()` via a `days_from_now(n)` helper, derive `cred_status` from the soonest expiry or compute it in the demo `list_row` with the tab thresholds, and derive `end_date_time` from `days_to_expiry` in the SSO fixtures. The proposed native test cannot run under `just web-test` as written (the demo module is feature-gated and imports js_sys), so make the row builders feature-free or use a wasm_bindgen_test.

- **F420 · Enterprise sub-tab and SSO demo fixtures are deliberately static per SP; making them args-aware via variant_index would add per-object variety** — `apps/desktop/web-rs/src/demo/mod.rs:450` · partially (demo/mod.rs:419 acknowledges "representative sample data shown for every SP", and the doc's args-aware rule targets detail commands whose pane would not switch, so this is an enhancement, not a violated rule). `get_sso_config` returns `sp-demo` for Datadog, Slack and GitHub alike with identical thumbprints, and every SP shares three assignments and roles. Register the eight sub-tab commands with `mock_each` keyed on the id via `variant_index`, pass the real SP ids into `sso_config`, and give non-SAML SPs an `sso_mode: None` variant so the not-configured state is demonstrated too.
- **F222 · System-proxy support for all Graph/Exchange/KeyVault/ARM traffic depends on tauri-plugin-updater's default features, not our reqwest declaration** — `Cargo.toml:29` · confirmed. `cargo tree` shows reqwest's `system-proxy` reached only through `tauri-plugin-updater`'s defaults, and none of the seven `Client::builder()` sites sets a proxy, so trimming the updater's features would silently break every Graph call behind an OS-configured corporate proxy with no test catching it. Add `"system-proxy"` to the workspace reqwest feature list with a one-line comment stating it is enabled on purpose; optionally a repo_invariants grep.
- **F250 · Bug template does not ask for the install format although updater behaviour differs per format** — `.github/ISSUE_TEMPLATE/bug_report.yml:36` · confirmed. README spends paragraphs on NSIS versus MSI conflicts and non-updating `.deb`/MSI installs, yet the template asks only for OS and a version with a "0.1.0" placeholder (the app is 0.30.2). Add a required "Install format" dropdown (NSIS, MSI, .dmg, .AppImage, .deb, source) and an optional "auto-update disabled?" checkbox, refresh the placeholder, and consider a `docs` label and template for the doc-drift items this review found.
- **F298 · Release guard re-runs cargo-audit but not cargo-deny, so `yanked = "deny"` is unenforced on the shipping lockfile** — `.github/workflows/release.yml:80` · confirmed (severity lowered because a `--locked` build still compiles a yanked crate, so this is consistency with stated intent rather than a shipping risk). The guard's own rationale (an advisory filed between merge and tag) applies equally to yanks and the `[bans]`/`[sources]` policy, but only `just audit`/`just web-audit` run and cargo-audit reports yanks as warnings. Add `cargo-deny@0.20.2` to the `install-action` list (keep the pin, `web-deny` depends on the 0.20 CLI) and `just deny`/`just web-deny` steps after the audit steps.
- **F309 · The release bump is hand edits plus hand-typed `cargo update`; a `just bump VERSION` recipe would remove the last hand-typed cargo from the release skill** — `.claude/skills/release/SKILL.md:37` · partially (`every_manifest_states_the_same_version` runs under `just test` on the release PR, so a partial bump fails PR CI rather than only after a tag push, and `--locked` in the original proposal would make `cargo update` refuse to rewrite the lockfile). The skill instructs the agent to hand-type `cargo update`, which AGENTS.md forbids, because no recipe exists. Add `just bump VERSION` rewriting the three literals, running `cargo update --workspace` in both trees and then `just test-crate desktop -- release`; point SKILL.md sections 1-2 at it.
- **F311 · `verify`'s closing summary omits `web-itest-size` from the gates it did not run** — `justfile:302` · partially ("near-free" holds only right after a `web-itest`; on the skip path it is a 45-50 s codegen build, and the gate is documented as CI and `verify-full` only). The summary names only audit and deny as not run, so a contributor on a browserless box believes `verify` covers everything it could. List `web-itest-size` in the summary line; optionally run it from the `[unix]` `web-itest-auto` only after a successful `web-itest`, never on the skip path.

---

## Documentation, tests, CI/tooling and accessibility

This bucket holds 103 verified findings about the repository's documentation, its test and invariant coverage, the CI and `just` tooling, and the frontend's accessibility. None is high severity; 31 are medium and 72 are low. Two pairs describe the same defect at the same file and are merged below (F313 with F362, F014 with F245), so the section has 101 entries. The medium items cluster in three places: architecture docs that now contradict AGENTS.md and the code, mutation-heavy surfaces with no behavioural test, and ARIA state that is conveyed by a CSS class alone.

### Docs drift: claim vs code, grouped by document

**AGENTS.md and the `.claude/rules` files**

- **F199 · AGENTS.md, the rules file, README and the Permissions tab header describe a bundled permissions catalog with Graph fallback; the code resolves every permission live** — `AGENTS.md:112` · confirmed. Four documents (AGENTS.md:112 and :48, `.claude/rules/backend-commands-and-caches.md:29`, README.md:75-77, `permissions_tab.rs:1-3`) say permission names come from a bundle with Graph as fallback, while `azapptoolkit-permissions` bundles only a six-entry resource directory and every permission is resolved via `resolve_resource_sp()` under `CacheKind::Permissions`. Reword all four to describe the resource directory plus live resolution.
- **F100 · AGENTS.md says `repo_invariants/trust.rs` pins bounded SAML cert lifetimes; it does not** — `AGENTS.md:122` · confirmed. trust.rs has exactly three rules (federation trust, redirect URI, retry class) and never mentions `resolve_cert_lifetime_days` or `MAX_CERT_LIFETIME_DAYS`; the bound is unit-tested only inside `sso/mod.rs`, and `.claude/rules/auth.md:14,22` repeat the claim. Either add a trust.rs rule that every `add_token_signing_certificate(` caller also calls `resolve_cert_lifetime_days(`, or reword the sentence to say where the bound really lives.
- **F241 · AGENTS.md is 2 bytes under its 20 000-byte budget; ~1.7 KB is duplicated verbatim elsewhere** — `AGENTS.md:20` · confirmed. `wc -c` gives 19 998 against `release.rs:115 BUDGET = 20_000`, so the next one-sentence invariant the file itself mandates fails a test; the Quick-reference Verify row, L176, the release-build sentence and L180 all restate text that already lives at their pointers. Delete or compress those passages (about 1 150 to 1 500 bytes) without dropping a rule.
- **F099 · Rules file forbids bare `invoke` while ten bindings use it by design and AGENTS.md/demo test sanction it** — `.claude/rules/backend-commands-and-caches.md:15` · confirmed. The rule says "never bare `invoke`" while AGENTS.md:146, `frontend.md:16` and `demo_fixture_coverage.rs` treat infallible `invoke::<()>` (e.g. `cancel_audit`) as sanctioned when a demo fixture exists. Reword line 15 to reserve the bare form for `()`/infallible commands that carry a `register_fixtures` entry.

**README.md, SECURITY.md and `.github` configuration**

- **F240 · SECURITY.md and the bug template direct users to a "Help → About" screen that does not exist; no security contact address** — `.github/SECURITY.md:10` · confirmed. - **F304 · dependabot.yml sha2 rationale is stale: the root lockfile already carries both sha2 0.10 and 0.11** — `.github/dependabot.yml:40` · confirmed. The comment says a bump "would add a second crypto major", but `Cargo.lock` already holds sha2 0.10.9 and 0.11.0 (via `secret-service 5.2.0`); the rules file and release-updater-demo.md carry the accurate nuance. Rewrite the ignore comment to match them (0.10 pinned by oauth2/p12-keystore/tauri-codegen, 0.11 Linux-only) and point at the architecture doc rather than restating.
- **F303 · codeql.yml comments describe CodeQL 2.25.6 and point at a branch that no longer exists; the bundle is now v2.27.0** — `.github/workflows/codeql.yml:49` · confirmed. The "revisit when the bundle advances" block cites 2.25.6 and branch `ci/codeql-proc-macro-expansion`, while `init`/`analyze` pin `codeql-bundle-v2.27.0` and `git ls-remote` shows only `main`. Re-run once on 2.27.0, record the call-target figure (or drop the caveat, including AGENTS.md:174), and replace the branch reference with a SHA or delete it.
- **F246 · README Logs section: the file glob does not match the real filenames and the 14-day retention is undocumented** — `README.md:396` · confirmed. `lib.rs:341-352` writes `azapptoolkit.YYYY-MM-DD.log` with `.max_log_files(14)`, so the documented `azapptoolkit.log*` matches nothing; the panic hook (`lib.rs:369-381`) also writes to that file. Document the real pattern, the 14-day retention and the panic capture.

**`docs/architecture` deep-dives**

#### F239 · medium · effort S · auth-and-consent.md carries two stale rationales that contradict AGENTS.md and the code
`docs/architecture/auth-and-consent.md:82` · confirmed
**Problem.** Lines 82-84 say the `BearerProvider` boundary "flattens errors to `String`", but `core::token::BearerProvider::bearer` returns `Result<String, TokenError>` and `token_adapter::token_error` maps `ConsentRequired` to the `consent_required` code, exactly as AGENTS.md pins. Lines 118-119 tell contributors to "extend BOTH `matches!` sets (`state.rs` + `shell.rs`)", yet no `matches!` exists in either file; `core::reauth::REAUTH_FATAL_CODES` is the single definition. AGENTS.md sends every auth contributor to this doc, so it currently instructs them to recreate the duplication the repo forbids. The same stale wording survives in `state.rs:557-559`.
**Proposal.** Reword lines 82-84 to the current reason for pre-acquisition (the `ensure_*` probe surfaces `consent_required` before side effects and keeps the CAE/non-CAE cache pairing in one place). Replace lines 118-119 with "add the code to `REAUTH_FATAL_CODES`; both `is_reauth_fatal` predicates read it", and fix the `state.rs` comment in the same commit.

#### F169 · medium · effort S · exchange-scoping.md still documents the deleted value-only scoping functions and a moved helper path
`docs/architecture/exchange-scoping.md:33` · confirmed
**Problem.** The doc names `exchange_role_for_permission` (:33) and `is_scopable_exchange_permission` (:49, :123-124) as the right tools for the probe and badge paths. `roles.rs:11-17` says the value-only forms were deleted, and `repo_invariants/commands.rs:130-134` fails the build if either name reappears. The :123-124 story about `held_orgwide_mail_grants` is also stale in substance, since `mail_scopes.rs:67-70` now filters with the resource-aware gate. Line 141 cites `client.rs::sanitize_error_body`, which moved to `client/transport.rs:293`.
**Proposal.** Rewrite :29-34 around `exchange_role_for_resource_permission` and `is_scopable_exchange_resource_permission`, state that the value-only forms were removed and link the invariant. Describe what `held_orgwide_mail_grants` does today and correct the transport path.

#### F397 · medium · effort M · Doc claim that adding a mechanism touches three places is wrong: the wizard branches in about ten places
`apps/desktop/web-rs/src/components/scope_wizard.rs:83` · confirmed
**Problem.** audit-findings-and-remediation.md:135-136 and `.claude/rules/scoping-and-audit.md:25` promise "add a `ScopeKind` variant + a target panel + a Step-3 apply arm, nothing else branches on the concrete mechanism". The wizard branches on `ScopeKind` at :83-89, :484-488, :507, :548-604, :655-659 and :782/:819/:828, and on the per-mechanism `ScopeMode` enum at :687, :698, :722, :734 plus the hint copy at :866. `ScopeKind::admin_applicable` is not referenced anywhere in web-rs, so the "seam" the doc mentions is unwired. A contributor following the doc gets two compile errors and silently misses the review strings, consent feature key, Step-1 Show block and radio.
**Proposal.** Correct both sentences to enumerate the real touch points (default_mode, preseed, toggle, Plan match, consent feature key, Step-1 Show block and radio, the four ScopeMode-driven review strings, the hint copy). A `MechanismSpec` table refactor would make the original claim true and is optional.

Beyond the `matches!` instruction (see F239), three docs cite `service.rs::` although the module is now `service/{mod,wire,loopback,scopes}.rs`, README.md:326/:473 credit libsecret and hwchen `keyring` while the crate uses `keyring-core` plus `zbus-secret-service-keyring-store`, and the `[`keyring`]` rustdoc links in `lib.rs:5` and `token_cache.rs:7` resolve to nothing. - **F191 · Crate-level docs misstate the retry set and claim ConsistencyLevel is added automatically** — `crates/azapptoolkit-graph/src/lib.rs:5` · confirmed. Rewrite the two bullets so a new `$count` query is not left silently ignored.

### Missing documentation

#### F236 · medium · effort S · README Features omits DR backup/restore, the Settings page, the tenant-wide SSO certificate board, launch session restore and the .pfx export
`README.md:39` · partially, the Bulk Actions page and per-app SAML cert health are already partly covered at README.md:59-60 and :97
**Problem.** A grep of README.md for backup, disaster, restore, tenant connection, SSO certificate, .pfx or session restore returns nothing. `ActiveView` has `DisasterRecovery`, `Settings` and `BulkActions` variants, `lib.rs` registers `backup_tenant`, `plan_restore`, `restore_tenant`, `list_sso_certificate_expirations`, `bulk_stage_sso_certificates` and `restore_session`, and a whole architecture doc covers DR. The README is the product page for the Releases audience, and its largest gap is the cross-tenant DR feature. The 0.30 micro-UX items (palette by name, arrow keys) are optional for a product README.
**Proposal.** Add a Disaster recovery bullet group cross-linking backup-and-restore.md, one bullet each for the SSO certificate expiry board with bulk staging, the Settings page and the `.pfx` output of Generate certificate, and fold launch session restore into the Quick start sentence about silent reconnect. Name the Bulk Actions page inside the existing bulk-sweep bullet.

#### F255 · medium · effort M · Capabilities catalog has no entry for the `policy_write` (claims mapping) and `sync` (provisioning) on-demand scopes
`crates/azapptoolkit-core/src/capabilities.rs:125` · confirmed
**Problem.** `CAPABILITIES` (:125-404) carries `scope_feature` values for write, audit_log, policy, sharepoint, group_membership, keyvault, arm, log_analytics and exchange only, while `state.rs:534-552 consent_scopes_for` also serves `"sync"` and `"policy_write"`. `check_readiness` builds its probes from the catalog, so neither scope is probed or shown, and no `forbidden_remediation` is spliced into the provisioning read or the SSO claims writes. Microsoft Learn confirms `Synchronization.Read.All` and the Application/Cloud Application/Hybrid Identity Administrator roles for the sync jobs read. The catalog header promises all three feedback mechanisms read from this one table.
**Proposal.** Add `saml_claims_mapping` (Plane::EntraDirectory, scope_feature `policy_write`) and `provisioning_status` (scopes `["Synchronization.Read.All"]`, scope_feature `sync`) rows with their roles. Add a test that every `consent_scopes_for` key has a catalog row, and splice `forbidden_remediation` into the SSO claims and provisioning commands.

#### F233 · medium · effort S · Entra custom role omits the directory permissions behind SAML cert rollover, claims mapping, SP delete/owners and My Apps visibility
`docs/operator-rbac/entra-custom-role.ps1:48` · confirmed
**Problem.** The script grants only `servicePrincipals/{create, allProperties/read, basic/update, appRoleAssignedTo/update, synchronization/standard/read}`. The app runs `addTokenSigningCertificate`, `remove_service_principal_key_credential`, `create/assign_claims_mapping_policy`, SP owner add/remove, `set_service_principal_tags` and `delete_service_principal` as the operator. Microsoft Learn lists `servicePrincipals/credentials/update`, `policies/update`, `applicationPolicies/basic/update`, `servicePrincipals/delete`, `owners/update` and `tag/update` as distinct permissions, so the inline comment that `basic/update` covers the HideApp tag is unsupported. An operator provisioned exactly per this file gets 403 on README-advertised SAML flows.
**Proposal.** Add the missing `servicePrincipals/*` and `applicationPolicies/*` actions to `$allowedResourceActions`, mirror them in the OPERATOR-ROLES.md section-2 table with the feature each unlocks, and fix the `basic/update` comment. The verifier notes that `applications/appRoles/update` is not on the custom-role list and `applicationPolicies/create` is not in the Learn scenario, so trim those two.

- **F281 · No accessibility statement despite substantial shipped a11y work** — `README.md:142` · confirmed. README, CONTRIBUTING, DEVELOPMENT.md and frontend-workspace.md never mention accessibility, screen readers or WCAG, although 0.30.0 shipped contrast, reduced-motion, colour-independent severity and arrow-key work, plus a shortcuts sheet. Add `docs/ACCESSIBILITY.md` (target level, keyboard model, known gaps, how to report) and link it from README's "App experience & operator tools" section and CONTRIBUTING's pre-PR checklist.
- **F235 · OPERATOR-ROLES.md never names two catalog features (DR restore, Key Vault RBAC reverse lookup) although their permissions are already covered** — `docs/operator-rbac/OPERATOR-ROLES.md:130` · partially, neither gap leaves an admin under-provisioned since the Azure JSON and caveat 1 already grant the privileges. `capabilities.rs:21` claims sync with this doc, yet `tenant_restore` and `keyvault_rbac_reads` are never named in its 200 lines. Add one sentence to caveat 1 naming DR restore as a re-consent path, add "Vault access" to the KeyVault table row, and optionally pin the sync with a repo_invariants test over `Capability.label`.
- **F137 · The module's PowerShell provenance contract is unverifiable in-repo and no rule catalog exists** — `crates/azapptoolkit-core/src/audit/mod.rs:3` · confirmed. Tests cite `Constants.ps1`/`Credential-Analysis.ps1` line ranges, but no URL, commit or vendored copy of the legacy module exists, and Rules 1-20 are never enumerated in any doc (only 4, 11, 12, 13, 14, 15, 18 are mentioned). Add a rule-catalog table to audit-findings-and-remediation.md and name the repository plus commit the citations refer to (or vendor the cited files if the module is public).
- **F249 · CHANGELOG.md has no title and no pointer to the archived releases** — `CHANGELOG.md:1` · confirmed. The file opens with `## [Unreleased]`, ends at 0.27.0, and mentions `docs/CHANGELOG-archive.md` only inside a 0.30.0 entry, so GitHub readers see history start at 0.27.0. - **F228 · time dependency is undocumented and leaks across the cert.rs boundary into a chrono-only codebase** — `apps/desktop/src-tauri/Cargo.toml:88` · confirmed. `time = "0.3"` sits under a heavily annotated crypto block with no rationale, and `GeneratedCert.not_after` exposes `time::OffsetDateTime`, forcing a manual unix-timestamp round trip at `credentials.rs:140-141`. Add a one-line comment (rcgen's validity fields are `time::OffsetDateTime`) and convert to `chrono::DateTime<Utc>` inside `cert.rs`.
- **F256 · Exchange client rides the undocumented `adminapi/beta/{tenant}/InvokeCommand` gateway while comments and doc links present it as the documented Admin API** — `crates/azapptoolkit-exchange/src/client.rs:22` · confirmed. The code still carries a pre-launch "verify during the live transport spike" note, cites the `adminapi/v2.0` get-started page for an endpoint that page does not describe, and nowhere records that the documented API exposes none of the cmdlets in `rbac.rs`. Replace the note with a paragraph stating what `InvokeCommand` is, what the documented surface covers, and the migration trigger; add the same to exchange-scoping.md.

### Test gaps by crate and tier

**Desktop backend (`src-tauri`): command handlers and repo invariants**

#### F088 · medium · effort M · IPC parity (generate_handler, #[tauri::command], bindings) has no CI pin, only an advisory hook that always exits 0 and needs jq
`.claude/hooks/command-parity-check.sh:15` · confirmed
**Problem.** A command missing from `generate_handler![]`, a misspelt invoke string or a mismatched arg-struct field compiles on both sides and fails only at runtime; the GUI tests mock IPC so they cannot catch it. The only guard is a PostToolUse hook that exits 0 always and skips silently without jq:
```
# Exits 0 always — this hook never blocks. The agent makes the judgment call.
if ! command -v jq >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  exit 0
```
Nothing under `repo_invariants/` or `web-rs/tests/` mentions `generate_handler` or the bindings, and ci.yml:217-223 says CI only shellchecks the hooks. The verifier re-derived 186 commands, 186 handler entries and 186 bound literals, so the check is mechanical and green today.
**Proposal.** Add `repo_invariants/ipc.rs` reusing `sources::commands()`: every command appears once in `generate_handler![]` and vice versa, every command has a `"name"` literal under `bindings/`, each `invoke*` arg struct's fields equal the command's non-`State` parameters (with `rename_all = "camelCase"` where needed), and the binding's `Result<T, UiError>` `T` matches the command's. Keep a vacuity floor of at least 150 commands and keep the hook as the fast local escort.

#### F068 · medium · effort S · The site tester's verdict assembly and its two pure helpers (roles_for_app, scope_for_level) have no unit tests
`apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands/permission_tester.rs:922` · partially, the ListItem/File level asymmetry is already pinned in `core/scoping.rs:940-965`, so the tester needs wiring tests only
**Problem.** The tests module (:1230-1535, 15 tests) covers synthesize, `rbac_reach_from_rows`, `classify_reacher`, merge and CSV only. No test calls `roles_for_app` (:922-933) or `scope_for_level` (:873-877), and `test_site_access`'s verdict assembly (:1077-1140) sits inline behind a `State`. These functions decide the SharePoint verdict, including the held-unreadable branch that a sibling finding shows is currently wrong; the Exchange half of the same file shows the intended test density.
**Proposal.** Extract `site_verdict(held, hit, label)` and table-test it: org-wide wins; entry plus matching scope gives scoped; entry without scope gives no_access naming `required_scope_for(level)`; unreadable gives unknown either way; no SP gives no_access. Add wiring tests that `roles_for_app` case-folds and dedupes and that `scope_for_level` picks a ListItem scope for a File hit and rejects the reverse.

#### F010 · medium · effort M · Only add_password_core has handler-level (AppState::for_test + wiremock) tests; the grant/create/owners tiering decisions are review-only
`apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands/permissions.rs:618` · partially, the grant cores take `&GraphClient` and the tiering decision lives in the `State`-taking commands, so the bodies must move into `_core(&AppState, ..)` first
**Problem.** `#[tokio::test]`, wiremock and `AppState::for_test` appear only in `applications/credentials.rs:456-597` and `backup.rs:1024`; `permissions.rs` and `applications/mod.rs` have none. `credentials.rs:26-35` explains that the rules living in handlers (call Graph, invalidate only on `Ok`, bust the right tier) were checked by review alone. The `sp_created`-to-lists-versus-detail tiering in both grant commands, `remove_expired_passwords`, `set_application_owners`, `downgrade` and `create_application` are exactly such rules, and two of them are wrong today per sibling findings.
**Proposal.** Refactor `create_application`, `grant_single_permission`, `grant_admin_consent`, `set_application_owners` and `remove_expired_passwords` into `pub(crate) async fn *_core(state: &AppState, ..)` bodies in the `add_password_core` shape. Add `handler_tests` modules mirroring credentials.rs: success busts the expected tier and keeps both indexes, failure busts nothing, partial success busts and returns the error.

#### F077 · medium · effort S · Rollover guard logic (five error codes) has no test coverage because it is only reachable through State-bound commands
`apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands/sso/mod.rs:946` · confirmed
**Problem.** `cert_is_active`, `cert_is_staged`, `cert_not_staged`, `cert_expired` and `cert_not_found` appear only at their definition sites (:936, :951, :955, :1248, :1254); no unit, GUI or repo-invariant test references them. auth-and-consent.md documents these guards by name, including the expired-but-nominated message variant and the idempotent activate (:1258-1260), while `build_rollover` beneath them is table-tested at :1949-2130. These guards are what stop an operator retiring the certificate that signs assertions today.
**Proposal.** Extract two pure functions beside `build_rollover`: `retire_target(roll, key_id) -> Result<&SigningCertDto, UiError>` and `activation_target(roll, thumbprint) -> Result<Option<&SigningCertDto>, UiError>` (None meaning already active). Call them from the two commands and table-test every code and both `cert_is_active` messages with the existing `sp_with`/`cred` fixtures.

#### F097 · medium · effort S · Trust/redirect validation rules check the validator at module granularity, not per command
`apps/desktop/src-tauri/tests/repo_invariants/trust.rs:41` · confirmed, though the verifier puts effort at M because helper resolution is needed
**Problem.** Both rules iterate `command_modules()` and test `src.contains(VALIDATOR)` where `src` is the whole module (:32-43, :91-102), while the cache, cancel and fan-out rules already scan per `sources::commands()` body. `sso/mod.rs` (18 commands) and `restore.rs` already contain the validator name, so a new command there that builds `ApplicationWebPatch {` or `FederatedCredentialRequest {` from raw input passes today. This is the helper bleed `sources.rs:123-131` was written to remove, on a security invariant.
**Proposal.** Rewrite both rules over `sources::commands()`: for each command body containing a `TRUST_WRITES`/`REDIRECT_WRITES` literal, require the validator or its module-local wrapper (`check_federated_credential(`, `checked_uris(`) in that body, resolving one level of local helper with `balanced_block`. Keep the `found >= 2` floors.

- **F029 · No source-level pin that the CacheKind::Audit write is guarded by run_is_cacheable** — `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands/audit.rs:445` · confirmed. `run_is_cacheable` is unit-tested, but nothing outside audit.rs references it and `repo_invariants/cache.rs` mentions `CacheKind::Audit` only in the detector's self-tests, so moving the `cache.put` out of the `if` passes every test. Add a `repo_invariants/cache.rs` rule that every `.put(CacheKind::Audit` sits inside an `if` whose condition contains `run_is_cacheable(`, with a non-vacuity floor.
- **F050 · `resolve()` precedence (env > settings.json > baked > placeholder) has no unit test, and DEVELOPMENT.md/.env.example omit the settings.json tier** — `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/state.rs:128` · confirmed. `state.rs` tests (:710-787) cover only `CancelFlag`, and the empty-string fall-through at each tier is untested; `.env.example:9-11` also omits the settings tier (see F238). Add table-driven tests (env wins, empty env falls through, empty settings falls through, empty baked falls to default) and fix the two docs.
- **F122 · `token_adapter::token_error` duplicates the `From<AuthError> for UiError` code table by hand with a 'must match' comment but no test pinning agreement** — `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/token_adapter.rs:19` · confirmed. `grep "token_error("` finds only the definition; a variant added on the dto side would silently fall to `"token_error"` here. Derive it (`let ui = UiError::from(err); TokenError::new(ui.code, ui.message)`, feasible since `crate::dto` re-exports `UiError`) or add a test asserting `token_error(e).code == UiError::from(e).code` over the constructible variants.
- **F104 · Retry-class rule walks only `crates/`, so a `with_retries` call site in the desktop crate is unscanned** — `apps/desktop/src-tauri/tests/repo_invariants/trust.rs:139` · confirmed. `trust.rs:139-142` walks `../../../crates` only; the desktop crate already hosts HTTP code (`cert.rs`, the `metadata_http_client()` probe, the reqwest pool) where a one-off wrapper could appear. Push `CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR/src` onto the walk, skipping `http_retry.rs` as today.

**Frontend (`web-rs`): browser GUI tests, native tests and demo fixtures**

#### F410 · medium · effort L · Mutation-heavy surfaces have zero GUI coverage: SSO wizard, federated credentials, Expose-an-API, create app, enterprise Access/App roles
`apps/desktop/web-rs/src/views/dialogs/sso_wizard_dialog.rs:171` · confirmed
**Problem.** A component-name grep over `tests/` returns zero mounts for 17 components, including `SsoWizardDialog`, `FederatedTab`, `ExposeApiTab`, `CreateAppDialog`, `ClaimsEditor` (945 lines), `AccessContent`, `AppRolesContent` and `PermissionsTab`. Of 183 command literals in `src/bindings`, 122 have no reference under `tests/gui`. The uncovered mutations are the ones that mint trust or do irreversible full-collection writes: `add/update/remove_federated_credential`, `set_identifier_uris`, `upsert_api_scope`, `set_pre_authorized_app`, `create_saml/oidc_sso_application`, `create_application`, and the enterprise access and app-role writes. AGENTS.md calls this suite the frontend's only behavioural gate.
**Proposal.** Add one GUI module per risk tier, in order: `federated_tab.rs` (auto-built issuer/subject, Remove goes through ConfirmDialog), `expose_api_tab.rs` (writes send the whole current state, failed save leaves the form editable), `sso_wizard.rs` (protocol to create for SAML and OIDC, show-once summary renders), then create app, enterprise access and app roles. Group them by the ApplicationDetailPane subtree, re-measure with `just web-itest-size`, and split a shard 5 if one crosses 52 MB.

#### F343 · medium · effort L · Browser GUI tests do not exercise the Owners, Expose an API or Permissions tab actions, nor rotate/new-secret/federated flows
`apps/desktop/web-rs/tests/gui/application_detail.rs:12` · confirmed
**Problem.** `application_detail.rs` has four smoke tests (load, notes, copy badge, retry), and per-string greps find no GUI test mentioning Downgrade, Grant admin consent, Revoke, Replace all, Add Default Owners, Add a scope or New secret. `permissions_tab.rs` has five native tests of pure helpers; `owners_tab.rs` and `expose_api_tab.rs` have none. A mis-wired `PendingRevoke` arm, a lost downgrade note or a wrong id in Replace-all would pass CI.
**Proposal.** Add `tests/gui/owners_tab.rs`, `expose_api_tab.rs` and `permissions_tab.rs` with mocked IPC: the confirm dialog names the row's subject and the mocked command receives the right ids, the downgrade chooser lists `downgrade_alternatives`, Replace-all sends exactly the staged ids, and the new-secret flow shows the reveal and reloads only on Done. Reuse the `certificate_reveal.rs` mount helpers and respect the per-shard ceiling (this pane lives in `gui_1.rs`).

#### F400 · medium · effort S · No GUI test renders a cancelled, truncated or degraded audit run, so the "never shown as all-clear" invariant is unpinned at the UI layer
`apps/desktop/web-rs/tests/gui/security_findings.rs:109` · confirmed

The partial-run disclosure is spread over the posture strip and two FindingsPane callouts, none of which the DTO's `is_complete()` unit tests can see. A sibling finding's truncated-with-findings gap would have been caught by one fixture with `truncated: true`.
**Proposal.** Add three cases to `security_findings.rs`: `cancelled: true` with items shows the strip's cancelled callout, `truncated: true` with zero findings shows the warn callout rather than "nothing to fix", and `degraded: vec![AuditCoverageGap::PerPrincipalScoring]` shows the "Part of this scan could not run" lede.

**`azapptoolkit-auth`**

#### F116 · medium · effort M · The interactive flow is untestable (hard-wired browser launch + internal listener), so sign_in's tid check, the nonce check, consent identity rejection and reauthenticate have no end-to-end test
`crates/azapptoolkit-auth/src/service/mod.rs:213` · confirmed
**Problem.** `run_auth_code_flow` binds a private loopback listener (:221-228) and calls `open_system_browser` unconditionally (:263), so no test can drive `sign_in`, `consent_for_scopes` or `reauthenticate`. The test module covers silent paths, `authorize_url` shape and `ensure_same_identity` in isolation only. Untested security branches include the nonce mismatch (:294-296), the `tid` check (:313-318), missing `tid`/`oid` (:306-321) and the `known_tenants` re-insert after validation (:671-673). The wiremock harness already exists; only the seam is missing.
**Proposal.** Inject the launcher: a `browser: Box<dyn Fn(&str) -> Result<()> + Send + Sync>` field defaulting to `open_system_browser`, with a `with_browser_opener` constructor. A test opener parses `redirect_uri`/`state`/`nonce` and connects to the loopback port with `GET /?code=…&state=…`, while wiremock's `/token` returns an id_token carrying the chosen `tid`/`oid`/`nonce`. Then test happy sign-in, wrong `tid`, nonce mismatch, consent as a different `oid`, and reauth restoring `known_tenants`.

The only lock test checks `Arc::ptr_eq` on the per-key lock, not behaviour, so moving the second cache check under the lock would silently turn page walks into refresh storms; the keyring splice hazard is already closed structurally, hence low. Add a wiremock test with `.expect(1)` joining eight same-scope futures, a sibling proving two scope sets make two POSTs, and a CAE test asserting the `claims=` form field.

**`azapptoolkit-graph`**

#### F185 · medium · effort M · Zero wiremock coverage for the scoped batch continuation, the SP signing-cert password sweep, the Prefer-header gallery fetch and several other client methods
`crates/azapptoolkit-graph/src/client/batch.rs:192` · confirmed
**Problem.** A literal grep over the 12 files in `client/tests/` finds zero hits for `batch_list_site_permissions`, `finish_paged_batch_scoped`, `list_all_application_templates`, `get_application_backup_json`, `remove_service_principal_key_credential`, `patch_application_expose_api`, `set_application_app_roles`, `odata.maxpagesize`, `APP_BACKUP_SELECT` and `"missing batch response"`. Each encodes a hard-won contract recorded in a block comment: the scoped bearer must ride page 2 of a batched site read (a real 403 bug, CHANGELOG.md:516-520), the cert removal must also drop the paired PFX `passwordCredential`, the `Prefer` header goes on the first request only, and a `$batch` envelope missing an id must yield `Protocol`.
**Proposal.** Add one focused wiremock test per contract: a sub-response with a nextLink whose page-2 mock requires `Authorization: Bearer sp`; a Sign/Verify/password triple asserting the PATCH body drops all three; `header("prefer", "odata.maxpagesize=2800")` on page 1 and `header_is_missing` on page 2; backup JSON `$select`/`$expand` without `consistencylevel`; `patch_application_expose_api` clearing a seeded `resource:` key; and a missing-id envelope mapping to `Protocol`.

#### F184 · medium · effort S · No graph-level regression test that a non-idempotent POST is NOT replayed after a 502 / connection reset (the addPassword double-mint bug)
`crates/azapptoolkit-graph/src/client/transport.rs:779` · confirmed
**Problem.** `retry_class_for` (:770-783) and the explicit `RetryClass::Idempotent` override on the `$batch` POST (batch.rs:248-263) have no behavioural test in this crate; grep of `client/tests` for `502`, `NonIdempotent` or `Idempotent` returns nothing, and `tests/transport.rs:11` covers only the 429 replay. The core unit test and the `every_retry_call_site_derives_its_idempotency_class` scan pin the policy and the call-site shape, but neither catches `retry_class_for` returning Idempotent for POST. The CAE once-only guard (:657-660) is also unpinned despite the module header's claim.
**Proposal.** Add three wiremock tests: `add_password` against a 502 mock with `.expect(1)` returns `Server` and is not re-sent; `get_organization` 502-then-200 succeeds; `batch_get_json` 502-then-200 on `/$batch` succeeds. Pin the CAE guard: a provider whose `bearer_with_claims` token is still challenged yields `Unauthorized` after exactly two requests.

The AGENTS.md:106 sentence is therefore slightly inaccurate and the proposed scan would fail on day one. **`azapptoolkit-exchange` and `azapptoolkit-keyvault`**

#### F215 · medium · effort S · Exchange's hand-rolled ExchangeError::ui_code / is_retryable has no conformance test: Token passthrough and the retryable set are unpinned
`crates/azapptoolkit-exchange/src/error.rs:64` · partially, the graph half is overstated because `GraphError` is a pure macro instance whose behaviour the arm and keyvault conformance tests already exercise
**Problem.** Only arm and keyvault have `tests/error_conformance.rs`. Exchange's `ui_code` (:64-81) is a hand-written table whose Token arm passes the re-auth-fatal code through, and its inline tests cover only `is_missing_object`, the membership body matchers and the two Forbidden hints. Fan-outs call `session.note_code(err.ui_code())` (sharepoint.rs:926, backup.rs:351, restore.rs:412), and `repo_invariants/fanout.rs:105-113` records that flattening to `token_error` once made `is_reauth_fatal` unfirable. The arm header's remark that graph has "a dedicated tests/ tree" is imprecise (graph's tests live in `src/client/tests/`).
**Proposal.** Add `crates/azapptoolkit-exchange/tests/error_conformance.rs` mirroring arm's: `is_retryable() == http_retry::is_retryable_code(ui_code())` for every variant with the set exactly `[throttled, server_error, network_error]`; every `REAUTH_FATAL_CODES` entry survives `ExchangeError::Token` and is not retryable; `Forbidden{had_diagnostics:true}.ui_hint()` equals the `exchange_rbac` remediation. Skip the graph file and reword arm's header.

#### F202 · medium · effort S · Key Vault client tests skip the paths that matter most: `nextLink` paging, the off-origin refusal, `exp`/`nbf` serialisation on the rotation write, the 1000-page cap, and PUT replay behaviour
`crates/azapptoolkit-keyvault/src/client.rs:285` · confirmed
**Problem.** The tests module (:274-393) contains exactly `list_secrets_returns_items`, `set_secret_puts_value`, `get_secret_reads_value`, `retries_on_429` and `unauthorized_surfaces_typed_error`. The `check_origin` refusal at :155-160 protects the vault bearer and is unpinned in this crate (only `core::net::same_origin` has unit tests), `MAX_PAGES` is untested in both keyvault and arm, and `optional_unix_timestamp_ser` (models.rs:150-163) has no test although `rotate_app_credential` always sends `expires: Some(e)`. A rename slip would silently produce never-expiring vault versions.
**Proposal.** Add wiremock tests: a two-page `nextLink` list returns all items with no `api-version` on page 2; an off-origin `nextLink` yields `Protocol` with no second request; `set_secret` with `expires` matches `{"value":..,"attributes":{"enabled":true,"exp":<secs>}}`; a self-referencing `nextLink` hits the page cap. Mirror the cap test in arm and add a `list_log_analytics_workspaces` test reading `customerId`.

- **F170 · set_management_scope_filter's pre-write refusals, ensure_management_scope's create path and the transport's 5xx/network paths have no wiremock tests** — `crates/azapptoolkit-exchange/src/client/rbac.rs:114` · confirmed. `client/tests.rs` mocks only 200/400/401/403/404/429, never names `New-ManagementScope`, and its three `set_management_scope_filter` tests cover post-write behaviour; the filter parser is well pinned in `targets.rs`, so only the refuse-before-cmdlet ordering and the terminal `Server`/`Network`/`Throttled` mappings are open. Add tests with `.expect(0)` on the cmdlet for both refusals, a Get-then-New sequence test, and 5xx mocks carrying `Retry-After: 0` to avoid real backoff.

### CI and tooling

#### F296 · medium · effort S · Docs-only CI detector skips the very tests that pin AGENTS.md, CHANGELOG.md and the commit hook
`.github/workflows/ci.yml:75` · confirmed
**Problem.** The `changes` job classifies `*.md`, `docs/*` and `.claude/*` as non-build, and the rust job's test step is gated on `code == 'true'`:
```
*.md|docs/*|LICENSE|LICENSE.*|.claude/*) : ;;  # docs / non-build
```
`repo_invariants/release.rs` pins exactly those files: CHANGELOG.md at :55, :179 and :323, AGENTS.md at :119 and :217, and `conventional-commit-validator.sh` at :228. A PR that only edits one of them merges green with `just test` skipped, and the failure surfaces on the next unrelated code PR or at release time. Commit db600f0 is a real docs-only CHANGELOG commit.
**Proposal.** Add an exceptions arm ahead of the docs globs: `AGENTS.md|CHANGELOG.md|.claude/hooks/*) code=true ;;` (optionally `.claude/settings.json`). Update the `changes` job comment and `.claude/rules/release-and-dependencies.md:23` to name the exceptions; `.claude/rules/*.md` can stay docs-only.

- **F310 · `rust` matrix job boots three runners on docs-only changes just to echo; other jobs skip at job level** — `.github/workflows/ci.yml:101` · confirmed. The rust job runs checkout plus a "Skip notice" step on ubuntu, windows and macOS with six per-step `if:` guards, while web, actionlint, audit and deny use a job-level `if` whose comments state a skipped job satisfies the required check. Move the rust job to the same job-level `if`, drop the per-step guards and the notice, and describe the job-level skip in the `changes` comment.
- **F297 · pages.yml never redeploys the demo when web-rs's path-dependency crates (or the root manifest they inherit from) change** — `.github/workflows/pages.yml:10` · partially, the CHANGELOG half is overstated because `build.rs` bakes only the current version's section by design, and most core-only changes do not alter what the fixture-driven demo displays. `paths:` lists only `apps/desktop/web-rs/**`, `justfile` and `pages.yml`, yet 49 web-rs files import `azapptoolkit_core` and the wasm compiles `azapptoolkit-dto`. Extend `paths:` with `crates/azapptoolkit-core/**`, `crates/azapptoolkit-dto/**` and the root `Cargo.toml`; leave CHANGELOG.md out.
- **F299 · tauri-cli `=2.11.5` is hand-pinned in three places with no test keeping them in agreement or within the locked `tauri` runtime** — `.github/workflows/release.yml:138` · partially, the proposal's `X.Y` equality rule is invented since the comments only promise `cli <= runtime`. `release.yml:138`, `scripts/setup.sh:57` and `scripts/setup.ps1:35` carry the literal while `Cargo.lock` has `tauri` 2.11.6, and every other mirrored literal in the repo has a `repo_invariants` test. Add a `release.rs` test extracting the three literals, asserting they agree and that the CLI version is `<=` the locked `tauri` version by semver.
- **F422 · demo_fixture_coverage scan has two blind spots: non-recursive bindings walk and comment-insensitive registration match** — `apps/desktop/web-rs/tests/demo_fixture_coverage.rs:102` · confirmed. `read_dir_sources` reads one directory level, so a `bindings/sso/` subdirectory would drop out of the scan, and `demo.contains(&format!("\"{c}\""))` accepts a quoted mention inside a `//` comment as a registration. Walk `src/` recursively (skipping `ipc_mock/` and `demo/`), strip line comments before matching, require a `mock_ok(`/`mock_each(`/`mock_err(` prefix or the `for cmd in [` array, and add a negative unit test for each.
- **F216 · CI clippy runs only the default set; a curated, mostly advisory pedantic pass would have caught the blocking keyring call and ~100 mechanical clones** — `justfile:237` · partially, several listed lints (`redundant_clone`, `future_not_send`, `derive_partial_eq_without_eq`, `needless_pass_by_value`, `map_unwrap_or`) are unsafe to gate under `-D warnings` and a redundant clone is runtime allocation, not wasm size. Both clippy recipes run the default set, `Cargo.toml:75-76` has only `[workspace.lints.rust]`, and `release.rs:27-45` diffs only that table. Add a non-gating `just clippy-pedantic` recipe for both trees; gate only the low-false-positive subset (`unused_async`, `ref_option`, `format_push_string`, `unnecessary_wraps`, `unused_self`, `cast_possible_wrap`) after fixing the existing sites and extend the lint-block parity test.
- **F218 · `just check` type-checks the frontend without the test harness that `web-clippy` gates, so a GUI-harness compile error skips the inner loop** — `justfile:251` · confirmed. Line 251 omits `--all-targets --features test-support`, which `web-clippy` (:273-274) uses, so `test_support/`, `ipc_mock/` and the four `gui_N.rs` shards are not type-checked until `just verify` reaches web-clippy; the recipe's own comment promises "every test target". Add the two flags to line 251 and note in the :243-246 comment that the shipped `cfg(not(feature))` paths are covered by `web-build`.
- **F300 · No rustdoc gate anywhere: 588 intra-doc links are never checked** — `justfile:293` · confirmed. `grep 'cargo doc\|RUSTDOCFLAGS\|rustdoc'` finds nothing outside `target/`, `_verify-core` has no doc step, and clippy does not run rustdoc lints, so a renamed item silently breaks the links AGENTS.md steers every agent through; whether any is broken today is unverified. Add `[workspace.lints.rustdoc] broken_intra_doc_links = "deny"` mirrored into web-rs, a `doc` recipe (`cargo doc --locked --workspace --no-deps` depending on `_stub-frontend-dist`), and wire it into `_verify-core` and the ubuntu CI leg.
- **F213 · No unused-dependency gate anywhere in CI or the justfile** — `justfile:329` · confirmed. `grep machete\|udeps` over `.github`, `justfile`, `docs/`, `deny.toml` and every `Cargo.toml` returns nothing, while the `deny` job already installs pinned tools via `taiki-e/install-action`. Add a `machete` recipe over the root and `apps/desktop/web-rs` to `verify-full` and the `deps` job, with `[package.metadata.cargo-machete] ignored = ["thiserror"]` in arm/graph/keyvault as the permanent form (the macro cannot be changed).
- **F225 · No unused-dependency gate in verify-full/CI; five dead declarations slipped through** — `justfile:355` · partially, the count is 5 unique manifest lines (anyhow and thiserror in src-tauri, tracing in keyvault, arm and web-rs), not 6, and the gate would catch only hygiene items. Same gap as F213 from the deps-hygiene reviewer: `audit`/`deny` gate advisories, licenses, sources and bans, but nothing checks that a declared dependency is used. Add a `just udeps` recipe (`cargo machete --with-metadata`), wire it into `verify-full` and the `deny` job so `verify_full_runs_every_gate_ci_runs` pins them, and ship the thiserror ignore from day one.
- **F248 · No keyless macOS/Linux packaging recipe, yet DEVELOPMENT.md says you can build for your host with the recipes below** — `docs/DEVELOPMENT.md:112` · confirmed. `build-windows` is the only keyless recipe; the macOS and Linux subsections name only the `-updater` variants, which fail without `TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY`, and DEVELOPMENT.md:72 even offers a hand-typed `cargo tauri build` that AGENTS.md forbids. Add `build-macos` and `build-linux` recipes mirroring `build-windows`, then fix DEVELOPMENT.md and the AGENTS.md Canonical commands sentence.

### Accessibility and UX consistency

The fe-a11y-ux reviewer's ordering is kept: fix the shared primitives first (FilterToggle, FilterChip, DetailLoadError, the date-range field, IconButton labels), because each fix there is one line that every call site inherits, then the view-level disclosures and menus. F329 (test gaps) is the pin that keeps these from recurring.

#### F312 · medium · effort S · aria-expanded bound to a bare bool in FilterToggle and the account-menu trigger (the documented boolean-attribute bug, two new instances)
`apps/desktop/web-rs/src/components/filter_toggle.rs:24` · confirmed
**Problem.** Both bindings render `aria-expanded=""` when open and omit the attribute when closed, so a screen reader hears no state for the filter drawer or the account menu (which also declares `aria-haspopup="menu"`):
```
aria-expanded=move || open.get()
aria-expanded=move || menu_open.get()
```
frontend-workspace.md:71-73 documents exactly this Leptos behaviour, and the string form is already used at `global_search.rs:205`, `application_list.rs:529`, `open_items_dock.rs:96` and `changelog_notes.rs:62`. Nothing pins it: no test references `aria-expanded`, `aria-selected` or `aria-pressed`, and no source scan checks aria bindings.
**Proposal.** Change both to `.to_string()`. Add a GUI assertion that the FilterToggle button has `aria-expanded="true"`/`"false"`, and a source scan (repo_invariants or `web-test-strings-check.sh`) rejecting `aria-[a-z]+=move || ident.get()` without `.to_string()`; `repo_invariants/commands.rs` already shows the precedent for a frontend source scan.

#### F315 · medium · effort S · FilterChip (14 sites) conveys its active facet by class only, no aria-pressed/selected state
`apps/desktop/web-rs/src/components/filter_chip.rs:48` · confirmed
**Problem.** The chip `<button>` carries `class`, `type`, `prop:disabled` and `on:click` and nothing ARIA; the only active signal is the `filter-chip--active` class. The facet chips are the primary filter control on all three list views, while the sort toggles in the same toolbar already do it right at `application_list.rs:526-529` with `aria-pressed`. No GUI test references `filter-chip`, so the change is test-safe.
**Proposal.** Add `aria-pressed=move || facet.with(|f| f == value).to_string()` to the primitive so all 14 sites inherit; `role="radiogroup"` plus `aria-checked` is the stricter alternative but needs a container the callers own. Add a GUI assertion beside the existing facet tests.

#### F318 · medium · effort M · Async load and mutation errors are not announced: 65 of 66 form-error renders and DetailLoadError have no live role; the 'Copied' confirmation is silent too
`apps/desktop/web-rs/src/components/ui/detail_load_error.rs:35` · confirmed
**Problem.** `DetailLoadError` renders `<div class=classes><Body1 class="form-error">…` with no live role (17 sites), `confirm_dialog.rs:93` and `copy_icon_button.rs:55` likewise; of 66 `form-error` sites only one unrelated Callout has a `role=` nearby. A failed save, a failed tab load or the copy confirmation therefore mutates the DOM silently and leaves a screen-reader user on a disabled button. The repo knows the pattern: `dr.rs` uses `role="alert"` three times, `toast.rs:11` documents alert versus status, and Callout accepts a `role` prop.
**Proposal.** Add `role="alert"` to the DetailLoadError root (17 sites inherit).
